The common-good round added integration and scope clarification; its adverse-use comparison and warrant limitation were inherited. A richer joint-formation mechanism does not alone establish the contested ethical identity. Ren's direction is already clear, and no further endorsement or unchanged compiler rerun would close the gap. The universal objective remains active and unachieved; this records the limit of the available argument without claiming impossibility or reopening completed conditional results.

**Common generation and universal practical significance, 7 October 2026; finish-line goal continues.** [The direct ethical continuation](../../../research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/RECURSIVE-WARRANT-INTEGRATION.md#10-can-shared-generation-ground-a-common-practical-good) tests shared causal conditions, supported joint formation and common practical authority as distinct claims within one unfolding. It develops the positive co-formation account, retains actual generative enrichment in the adverse-use comparison, and tests completed practice with supported succession. A focused Spinoza rereading and root-authored substantive counterreview are integrated. **Result:** universal ethical validity need not serve every generated purpose; neither shared usefulness nor a bargain advantageous to all supplies the first ethical ground. The next central work concerns the differentiated quality of actual realization and its practical articulation, including conflicts across scopes of 生. No new theorem or compiler run; prior source-bound Lean results retain their scope. The universal and stronger no-assumptions requirements remain incomplete and the goal stays active.

**Universal mathematical completion requested, 7 October 2026; not achieved.** [The renewed argument assessment](../../../research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/ROOT-MATHEMATICAL-FAMILY-WARRANT-REASSESSMENT.md#6-renewed-completion-request-final-assessment-of-the-available-argument) separates full-domain admission, coverage of every mathematical subject and exclusive constitutive priority. The available positive argument does not discharge these obligations. Earlier Lean-checked reconstruction and assembly results remain valid at their stated scopes; no new theorem, compiler run or proof of impossibility is claimed. The stronger root claim remains a conjecture.

**Recursive formation and the universal finish line, 7 October 2026.** [The deeper integration](../../../research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/RECURSIVE-WARRANT-INTEGRATION.md) connects a positive ethical argument, substantive rival, two complete primary-paper readings and a fresh Lean check of actual output → reconstruction of selection arrangements → successor operation. **Eight new theorems / thirteen new declaration audits** establish a finite, resource-accounted connection; conversion can reverse which arrangement becomes prevalent despite the stronger source's greater output. Reconstruction laws and finite stocks remain supplied. Experiments support evolved collective recurrence, with laboratory conditions explicit. **Next central inference:** why forming conditions of further differentiated life has favorable quality and situated practical significance without requiring endorsement, retaining finite completion and [Ren's future-horizon correction](../../../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-07-generativity-future-horizon.md). Formation and retention of the selection arrangement remain supporting evolutionary work. This is a clearer route, not completed universal grounding or evidence of imminent proof.

**新形成关系 → 有支持的共同实现，2026年10月7日；接续轮完成。** [生成能力研究 §7–10](../../../research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/GENERATIVE-CAPACITY-INTEGRATION.md#7-从新增途径到共同实现)新增35项 Lean 定理、79项声明审计和1,109条独立对应记录：两种结构原本各自可形成，新形成的可复用连接使它们在相同支持下沿同一历史共同完成，包含最初形成成本。任意有限反复使用的优势、保留材料的中断比较、修复耗尽与普通路径恢复均受检查。伦理论证接上[Ren 的未来视域澄清](../../../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-07-generativity-future-horizon.md)，不再将永久不用能力的独立价值作为必经证明；共同实现的增益也不等于全部后果更善。**下一连接：**把另一种形成及连接重组纳入同一历史，检验共享形成与专门化造成的实际机会变化，再辩护整体方向和实践根据。普遍目标不变。

**Whole-family warrant reassessed, 7 October 2026; no new theorem.** [The direction audit](../../../research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/ROOT-MATHEMATICAL-FAMILY-WARRANT-REASSESSMENT.md) clarifies that the universal target may seek one common explanation of an entire domain, without a separate finite recipe for each member. Three collaborating audits find that the proposed binary-branching route repeats the existing history-completion and finite-refutation results; it supplies no new warrant that local formation exhausts whole-history admission. The existing finite-program theorem concerns a single complete output per description and cannot by itself refute a collective domain explanation. No new proof package or compiler run was added. Further progress requires an independently defended whole-range principle or a substantive constitutive-dependence argument; full admission and root exclusivity remain open.

**Indexed mathematical assembly, 7 October 2026.** [The new integrated argument](../../../research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/ROOT-MATHEMATICAL-ASSEMBLY-INTEGRATION.md) defends relative admission through abstraction of an independently constituted witness relation. A checked quotient retains exactly its output membership; an explicit parity recurrence calibrates formation without a target-predicate parameter. On `Nat`, finite admission plus closure under every increasing finite chain remains equivalent to fullness. Fresh Lean verification passes **31 new theorems and 48 audits**. Whole-relation admission, abstraction closure and inclusion in the intended predicate sort remain explicit. **Next universal bridge:** independently justify the required range of whole relations and its higher-level admission; countable indexing alone does not reduce that obligation. Affirmative argument, matched-rival review and focused Feferman–Strahm assessment are integrated.

**Recursive generative selection, 7 October 2026.** [Ren's evolutionary proposal](../../../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-07-recursive-generative-selection.md) now has an [integrated positive argument](../../../research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/RECURSIVE-GENERATIVE-SELECTION.md), [counterreview](../../../research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/RECURSIVE-GENERATIVE-SELECTION-REVIEW.md), [three-primary-source assessment](../../../research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/RECURSIVE-GENERATIVE-SELECTION-SOURCES.md) and [checked Lean comparison](../../../research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/RECURSIVE-GENERATIVE-SELECTION-MATH.md). Support-rich collectives' greater output can coexist with either global loss or global increase of the contributing variant under a specified production/transmission law. The [current theory](../../../../theories/shengsheng-buxi-generative-existence/unified-2026-10-05/GENERATIVE-SELECTION.md#recursive-generative-selection-across-scales) therefore develops recursive dependence and conditionally transmitted selection pressure, preserving the stronger hypothesis that selection can favor arrangements keeping future selection connected to renewal. **Next:** test actual recruitment after restoration, inherited association and selection of those arrangements. This supports the universal ethical inquiry without identifying evolutionary success with warranted good or treating every scale as a reproducing population.

**生成能力的形成、实现与接续，2026年10月7日；首轮构造与审查完成。** [本轮整合](../../../research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/GENERATIVE-CAPACITY-INTEGRATION.md)将上轮设计推进为实际结构、源批次和工作条件共同参与的组装规律：38项新 Lean 定理区分任意深的有限终末产物与同一历史中的反复形成，检查新结构中的接入位置、无限积蓄对照和有限供给边界。独立节点图重建与 Lean 的174条状态记录一致。伦理论证辩护新增有效形成途径本身的正面意义，保留实际未用／不可激活、有限完成及跨范围冲突。**下一连接：**新形成关系怎样扩大同样支持条件下的有效形成途径，及这一扩展何以构成有实践意义的更多生；当前固定组装规则中的深度增长还不是该扩展，更不是普遍伦理证明。

**整体中的有限情境判断，2026年10月7日；单独授权的伦理研究。** [本轮整合](../../../research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/SITUATED-WARRANT-INTEGRATION.md)汇合正面论证、保留整体关系的反评审及五项原始来源核查，推进 **整体生成质量 → 有限认识下的差异化实践根据**。它将当下实现、能力与依赖、传播、选择变化及认识条件接入同一帮助比较，并区分未知对部分理由、整体排序和实施许可的不同影响。及时可修订仍不足以授权集中试错负担；证据筛选失真不必等具体灾难出现才构成质疑。**同日选题修正：**回应 [Ren 对对立式框架的质疑](../../../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-07-situated-nondual-framing-question.md)，[当前中心问题](../../../research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/SITUATED-WARRANT-INTEGRATION.md#10-从共同生成重新选择中心问题)回到不同回应怎样共同形成生活、理解与后续条件，以及这些实现何以产生情境理由。风险与许可保留为实际关系分化后的局部检验，不再预设为基本入口。本轮无新数学证明或实验，未重跑既有检查；第一伦理识别、风险分配根据及普遍元伦理仍未完成。[Ren 的转述原话及来源范围](../../../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-07-situated-ethics-under-uncertainty.md)独立保存。

**Continuation depends on generativity, 7 October 2026.** [Ren's proposal and instruction](../../../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-07-continuation-depends-on-generativity.md) now have an [integrated affirmative argument](../../../research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/CONTINUATION-GENERATIVITY.md), [counterreview](../../../research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/CONTINUATION-GENERATIVITY-REVIEW.md), [three-source assessment](../../../research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/CONTINUATION-GENERATIVITY-SOURCES.md) and [checked Lean interface](../../../research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/CONTINUATION-RENEWAL-MATH.md). Under explicit well-foundedness and a renewal-or-descent split, an endless history renews arbitrarily late; a rank specialization bounds the next renewal. Fixed-form renewal remains possible, so novelty and complexity growth do not follow from these premises. The [canonical account](../../../../theories/shengsheng-buxi-generative-existence/unified-2026-10-05/UNIFIED-ETHICAL-GROUNDING.md#continuation-depends-on-generativity) preserves connected care, rest, actual losses and constructive endings. **Next universal inference:** justify the favorable aspect and practical scope of further differentiated formation beyond renewal needed to avert failure. The universal ethical proof remains open; the earlier abiotic test is retained.

**生成能力怎样继续生成，2026年10月7日；新判准的具体推进设计。** 回应 Ren 对新方向的提问，[研究稿 §6](../../../research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/SHENGSHENG-DIRECTION-RECONSTRUCTION.md)提出比较任意复杂的一次形成与沿同一历史实际重建后续形成条件，并检验新能力自身是否构成更丰富的生。首个 Lean 设计将跟踪实际组织依赖、材料和接口；现有有限分支定理不能独自保证无限路径上持续形成新结构。另一个伦理比较检验中心供给与新关系参与生成，保留两者均可能建设或耗损的情形。此处是研究设计，尚无新增定理；普遍伦理识别仍与机制对应一起受检验。

**生生不息的正面伦理方向，2026年10月7日；新实质输入改变前轮处境。** [Ren 的原话及范围澄清](../../../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-07-shengsheng-ethical-direction.md)将整体持续创造更多生、产生更丰富的可能性和多样性、使复杂组织能够继续发展的方向指认为善。多个“生”的范围都成立为研究对象，不能以旧 AI 限定否决。[当前重构](../../../research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/SHENGSHENG-DIRECTION-RECONSTRUCTION.md)恢复5月2日直接原话的正面谱系，并提出无生物生命区域中实际组织生成的判准比较，再研究其与生态、生活及心智范围的关系。完整普遍目标不变；本轮保存与发展候选，不计作新 Lean 定理或完成证明。

**Full admission and function-graph reconstruction, 7 October 2026.** [The integrated continuation](../../../research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/ROOT-MATHEMATICAL-ADMISSION-INTEGRATION.md) proves that admitting all finite predicates and every ambient nonempty directed union is equivalent to full predicate admission. A proper finite/cofinite range passes finite Boolean, finite-pattern and arbitrary-renaming tests, so these weaker conditions do not warrant fullness. Both predicates and arrows now have completion representations throughout the declared finite simple-type language, preserving all its formulas. Fresh Lean verification passes **52 new named theorems and 86 declaration audits**. All qualifying ideals, global graph totality and the all-family closure range remain explicit. **Next universal bridge:** independently justify that admission range and its constitutive root dependence; another equivalent representation does not discharge it. The affirmative argument, matched-rival counterreview and focused Feferman assessment are integrated.

**Universal first-warrant audit, 7 October 2026; historical impasse before Ren’s subsequent criterion.** [The latest three-reviewer assessment](../../../research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/UNIVERSAL-METAETHICS-LEAN-INTEGRATION.md#13-latest-constitutive-tests-and-the-current-research-impasse) checks genuine attenuation, shared appraisal through transformation and the existing Lean preservation results. It credits the substantive correction that capacity or earlier-purpose preservation is not automatically good, while finding no new universal ethical inference. The same first-warrant dependency persisted through the new evolution interface, actual-capacity audit and latest constitutive test. Further progress needs a substantive defense or discriminating evidence for the proposed whole ethical identification; another mechanism or conditional transport theorem does not discharge it. This is an impasse of the current run, not a proof of impossibility or a change to the repository's universal normative priority. Other authorized studies continue at their declared scopes.

**Formation, participation and responsibility named directly, 7 October 2026; editorial correction.** [Ren’s terminology request](../../../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-07-generative-language-and-formation.md) is implemented in the [shared method](../../../../theories/shengsheng-buxi-generative-existence/unified-2026-10-05/NONDUAL-METHOD.md#formation-participation-and-responsibility) and [current-language revision](../../../research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/LANGUAGE-AND-FORMATION-CORRECTION.md). Contextual wording distinguishes how conditions form, practical participation and self-direction, and justified responsibility. General mechanism summaries name resource requirements; actual economic relations and source terminology retain their meaning. This makes the disputed formation-to-warrant inference explicit; it supplies no new theorem or universal ethical ground.

**Conditional higher-order semantic reconstruction completed, 7 October 2026.** [The integration](../../../research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/ROOT-MATHEMATICAL-HIGHER-ORDER-INTEGRATION.md) proves all-formula truth preservation through finite-subset ideal representations for full relational second-order semantics and the declared finite simple-type language. The latter recursively completes predicate types and transports full function spaces, with lambda/application, typed equality, membership, quantification at every represented type and semantic comprehension. A checked finite-predicate counterexample retains the fullness distinction. Fresh Lean 4.29.1 verification passes 43 new named theorems and 97 declaration audits; standard dependencies exposed, prior proof assets retained. **Next universal bridge:** independently justify full predicate/function admission and its constitutive root dependence. The previously missing semantic correspondence is now completed at these scopes; universal grounding remains open.

**Shared appraisal through transformation, 7 October 2026; supporting interpretation study.** The [慧根 continuation](../../../research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/HUIGEN-SELF-GROUND-AUDIT.md#69-shared-appraisal-through-transformation) preserves Ren's mutual-benefit case: helping can partly constitute the worthwhile life the helper is becoming, so changed life-shape alone is no additional harm. A [new primary comparison](../../../research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/HUIGEN-SELF-GROUND-SOURCES.md#65-moral-disagreement-across-learning-histories) tests how different learning histories can concern one ethical question. The account must distinguish correction, changed subject matter and changed grounds; neither causal regulation nor common vocabulary settles this. This supports ethical appraisal without deriving its first warrant. The normative-grounding priority and existing conditional results remain unchanged.

**Evolved discernment → ethical warrant, 7 October 2026; actual capacity audited, universal proof open.** Ren's [generative-evolution direction](../../../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-07-liangzhi-generative-evolution.md) supplies a possible evidential route. [The universal-branch assessment](../../../research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/LIANGZHI-NORMATIVE-WARRANT-INTERFACE.md#the-actual-successor-already-available) now credits the existing actual successor's inherited influence, new evidence, corrected command and action, and the later implemented fulfillment-sensitive withdrawal. These are concrete conditional achievements, not generically missing reconstruction. Their ethical grounds and the claim interpretation remain supplied; formation evidence and successful application do not independently establish them. **Next central obligation:** defend the whole-relational ethical identification and the calibration hypothesis that would make formation/transmission evidence bear on it. Further mechanism composition supports the Liangzhi branch at its declared scope; it cannot replace this session's universal target. No new theorem, empirical run or universal ethical inference is claimed by the audit.

**良知的实践分殊与实际支持交接，2026年10月7日；新增20项 Lean 实查。** 依 [Ren 的生成演化方向](../../../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-07-liangzhi-generative-evolution.md)及生生正面判准，[最新论证、原典与反审整合](../../../research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/LIANGZHI-PRACTICAL-INTEGRATION.md)解释手段误认、目的改变、知情拒绝和既有承担怎样改变回应。实际有限模型形成、接通并运行替代支承，让原支承在活动完成前退出；保留真实失败／恢复、先撤后接也成功及无替代的有限完成。20项新增声明、4模块65项审计通过；局部规律与伦理解释范围明列。交接可行不等于整体更善，依存不独自分配责任。[当前理论](../../../../theories/shengsheng-buxi-generative-existence/unified-2026-10-05/LIANGZHI-EVOLUTION.md)继续保留19项传递、17项发展及前轮两项幼儿研究，未将它们拼为完整生物史。**下一连接：**目的／参与变化与真实依存冲突时，生生方向怎样确定理由范围及其辨别校准；普遍伦理证明未完成。

**Universal mathematical coverage and necessary grounding tested, 7 October 2026; both remain uncompleted.** [The direct completion attempt and integration](../../../research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/ROOT-MATHEMATICAL-COMPLETION-INTEGRATION.md) examine a global candidate with full profile admission and higher levels, then test necessary common dependence against a matched nondual structural rival. Fidelity to an admitted full subject does not yet justify its admission; shared mathematical content does not yet establish shared constitutive priority. The positive extension proves an executable evaluator for every first-order sentence in pure equality/order across supplied dense linear orders without endpoints. A separate diagonal package checks the exact obstruction to fixed self-indexed coverage and a faithful extension. Fresh Lean checks: 47 new named theorems, 79 declaration audits. **Next universal bridge:** derive an admission principle and defend its dependence from the root; another complete local fragment alone will not settle either universal clause. Ren's explicit mathematical request and the separately authorized normative programme retain their respective scopes.

**Whole realization → universal practical grounding, 7 October 2026; co-formation and completion scope integrated.** [The latest continuation](../../../research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/WHOLE-PRACTICAL-GROUND-INTEGRATION.md#11-co-formation-and-the-scope-of-the-universal-claim) connects the new actual joint-formation result, recursive selection and support handoff to the first-warrant question. The positive identity is sharpened as formation of compatibility among differentiated modes of becoming, with response participating in that realization. Ren's horizon correction removes permanent nonuse as an imposed gate; the sharp abiotic comparison separately tests the broad intrinsic extension, rather than defining universal meta-ethics by itself. The broad positive proposal remains active. Fresh independent reviews find genuine descriptive and application progress, but no completed warrant for the ethical identification or its full practical reach. Existing proofs retain their own receipts and supplied correspondence; this integration adds no theorem or empirical result. The present automated universal-warrant attempt has reached a specifically reviewed research impasse after three continuations; a new substantive argument or relevant evidence is needed to move that first conclusion, while supporting research remains authorized.

**王阳明整体整合完成，2026年10月7日。** 按 Ren「先完成王阳明」，[当前主文](../../../../theories/shengsheng-buxi-generative-existence/unified-2026-10-05/WANG-YANGMING.md)将一体之仁、明德亲民、知行与工夫重构为有伦理内容的同一展开；原典的本然良知和造化强说完整保留。实践形成的目的、理解及整个后续生活进入评价，苏秦张仪及适应性控制竞争者阻止把成功影响等同仁。拒绝、自护与合理结束明确作为有待具体论证的现代扩展。[来源、反审及整合](../../../research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/WANG-YANGMING-COMPLETION-INTEGRATION.md)与新增12项实际通过的 Lean 声明共同收束本轮；其中8项无命名公理依赖，其余仅使用明列的标准逻辑依赖。形式接口检验同一完整有限历史上的表示与共同响应条件，`Fits` 的伦理根据仍给定。**下一根本推论：**仁的整体实现何以具有跨不同智能形成史的实践效力；无假设普遍证明未完成。6日比较的独立患者／行动者主线作为历史分解保留，由本轮整体重构取代。

**Universal-metaethics scope and first-warrant audit, 7 October 2026; goal remains active.** [Three parallel reviews and the integration](../../../research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/UNIVERSAL-METAETHICS-LEAN-INTEGRATION.md#7-scope-and-prospective-ground-adjudication) correct an AI-authored narrowing: universal applicability and endorsement-independent validity do not prescribe positive assistance at every eligible position as the only possible conclusion. That stronger candidate remains unproved and preserved. The [canonical scope](../../../../theories/shengsheng-buxi-generative-existence/unified-2026-10-05/UNIFIED-ETHICAL-GROUNDING.md#universal-scope-and-substantive-conclusions) restores **whole qualitative realization → differentiated practical significance** as the central connection. [The prospective review](../../../research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/PROSPECTIVE-GROUND-CONSTITUTION-ARGUMENT.md) removes a temporal objection but supplies no new first warrant. **Next:** explain which actual fulfillments and obstructions have ethical character and how that same relationship grounds differentiated response; preserve the existing co-grounding argument as substantive support. The Lean package remains 39 previously checked theorems. No new ethical proof, mathematical result or empirical validation is claimed.

**Unique mathematical constitution pursued, 7 October 2026; checked advance, stronger claim open.** [Ren explicitly redirects this conversation to the stronger claim](../../../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-07-pursue-unique-mathematical-constitution.md). [The integrated round](../../../research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/ROOT-MATHEMATICAL-UNIVERSALITY-INTEGRATION.md) checks a general witness-adequacy criterion and least closure under fixed witness selectors, then proves that unrestricted correct selection can recover every sentence's truth through two named tags. The positive counterpart gives an executable evaluator for every sentence in pure equality using only finite equality patterns and explicit freshness. Fresh Lean verification: 43 named theorems, 84 declaration audits, two modules; standard logical dependencies exposed, no custom axioms or holes. This advances truth adequacy without identifying it with universal coverage or unique ultimate grounding. **Next:** independently derive adequate witness principles for richer languages and compare their explanatory dependence; merely supplying correct selectors does not reduce truth content. The separately authorized normative priority remains active.

**Situated good and generative trajectories, 7 October 2026; agreement-ground attempt revised.** [The new integration](../../../research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/PERSPECTIVAL-AGREEMENT-GROUND-INTEGRATION.md) joins substantive counterreview, correspondence review and two primary-source challenges. Settling a shared continuation supports a candidate reason for fidelity beyond specified material losses, but does not itself establish directed duty or release and enforcement powers. The review distinguishes the value of a commitment capacity, its valid exercise and its selected continuation; a new shared valuable output is not required by every valid promise. Legitimate finality and chosen lasting constraints remain possible. **Next supporting bridge:** particular entrusted decision → justified directed claim and its scope. The [canonical account](../../../../theories/shengsheng-buxi-generative-existence/unified-2026-10-05/UNIFIED-ETHICAL-GROUNDING.md#agreement-can-change-a-claim-without-establishing-improvement) preserves the earlier ratification and prospective-control findings; the broad allegiance rival is not newly defeated. No new theorem or empirical intervention; [25 checked correspondence results](../../../../experiments/valuation-development/lean/perspectival/README.md) remain unchanged. This supporting clarification does not reverse the universal first-warrant impasse.

**Universal-metaethics Lean audit, 7 October 2026; formal round verified, full goal active.** [The integration](../../../research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/UNIVERSAL-METAETHICS-LEAN-INTEGRATION.md) records fresh Lean 4.29.1 checks of 39 theorems and 61 declarations, all with empty axiom dependencies. The [package](../../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/universal-ethics/README.md) formalizes arbitrary-domain warrant propagation and compatible same-history composition; a separate audit identifies the exact double-negation dependency in an unrestricted no-cut upgrade. The inherited classical theorem is preserved. [The affirmative study](../../../research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/UNIVERSAL-RESPONSE-COMPOSITION-ARGUMENT.md) tests prospective imposition before adopted roles, while preserving the stronger injury/assistance rival. **Next-step selection qualified by the scope audit above:** prospective ground and the formation of dependence are supporting tests within whole ethical quality and differentiated practical significance; neither a supplied transport premise nor a successful local case completes the universal goal.

**Whole continuation and justified revision, 7 October 2026; supporting round complete.** [The integrated argument, counterreview and primary-source assessment](../../../research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/WHOLE-CONTINUATION-WARRANT-INTEGRATION.md) distinguish relief, justified bearing of a burden, legitimate finality or jurisdiction, and correction of an earlier ethical appraisal. Candidate preservation must allow warranted correction rather than fix every current verdict. A final genealogy audit finds the universal permitted-response argument returns to the existing constitutive-answer frontier: favorable whole quality → baseline practical favor beyond assent remains substantively defended, not newly derived. **Next:** an independent sufficiency argument, a substantive failure of exclusive role dependence, or a discriminating correction of ethical identification; repeating the same clean case is not a new advance. No new mathematical theorem, empirical result or universal ethical proof is claimed; the checked Lean completion package remains unchanged.

**Recovery rate, formation draw and starting stock derived, 7 October 2026 (Lean 4.29.1, 82 empty audits):** Three inputs the [contact-formation track](../../../research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/CONTACT-FORMATION-LAW.md) listed as supplied are now stated against the native port. [Recovery rate](../../../research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/RECOVERY-QUANTUM.md): a failed native conversion dissipates exactly ten; one pair per recorded failed conversion holds exactly at rate ten (`rate_determined`), and rates 9 and 11 fail in executed histories. [Formation draw](../../../research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/FORMATION-DRAW.md): the native commands share one per-operation draw; any single draw law of the extended process forces formation and recovery to draw `bill`, and a free formation has none. [Starting stock](../../../research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/STARTING-STOCK.md): zero spare pairs is forced by backing formations with failures in the same history; the closure history's least start is 114702 held and 20 source, attained and tight. Still supplied: the conversion unit ten and tariff themselves, the one-pair-per-failure and one-draw-law claims (each proved equivalent to the derived value), and that anything is held at all (proved necessary). With the process-law closure, 41 modules and 876 audits are empty.

**Universal meta-ethics of all intelligence — session goal clarified, 7 October 2026; unresolved.** [Ren expressly sets this objective](../../../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-07-universal-intelligence-session-goal.md), distinguishing it from valid bounded generative work. [The canonical target](../../../../theories/shengsheng-buxi-generative-existence/unified-2026-10-05/UNIFIED-ETHICAL-GROUNDING.md#session-target-universal-meta-ethics-of-all-intelligence) requires accounting for interconnectedness and the formation of agency within the whole. [The revised universal agenda](../../../research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/NORMATIVE-WARRANT-INTEGRATION.md#universal-derivation-now-governs-this-session) makes whole generative quality → universal practical significance the central inference. The former undertaking/answerability next round is subordinate as a test; bounded results cannot become this session's completion criterion. Other authorized sessions retain their scope. This records a governing research direction and philosophical argument, not a new universal theorem.

**Whole continuation and constructive endings, 7 October 2026; scope corrected.** [Ren corrects the subject-centered use of a persistence example](../../../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-07-whole-continuation-counterexample-scope.md): ask whether ending an activity can improve the whole continuation. [The canonical correction](../../../../theories/shengsheng-buxi-generative-existence/unified-2026-10-05/UNIFIED-ETHICAL-GROUNDING.md#ending-an-activity-and-the-quality-of-the-whole-continuation) and [naturalism scope note](../../../research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/ETHICAL-NATURALISM.md#111-取消活动与整体延续的评价范围) retain `loss_then_persistence` as a narrow compatibility result; it does not evaluate whole goodness, and its code contains no primitive subjects. **Next:** compare the actual whole continuations of retaining, transforming and ending the common undertaking, including realization, losses, impairment and subsequent conditions, and defend their qualitative appraisal. This renews the governing nondual method; no new theorem, experiment or universal ethical derivation is claimed.

**First research priority — universal normative grounding, 7 October 2026; status: active.** [Ren explicitly directs parallel deep investigation and repository-wide priority](../../../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-07-normative-grounding-first-priority.md). The [canonical programme](../../../../theories/shengsheng-buxi-generative-existence/unified-2026-10-05/UNIFIED-ETHICAL-GROUNDING.md#first-priority-universal-normative-grounding) investigates whole generative organization and realization → justified ethical quality → practical reasons and constraints, including candidate invariants through learning and self-modification. Mechanism, valuation-development, empirical, formal and alignment studies support this priority when they identify the premise, counterexample, correspondence or implementation condition they address. Conditional completion remains accepted; universal ethical necessity and the stronger no-assumptions objective remain unresolved. Concurrent valuation-development and enacted-orientation advances below are preserved at their declared scopes; neither claims to establish that persistence or acquired valuation is good.

**Supporting completion proofs, checked 7 October 2026:** [the Lean integration](../../../research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/COMPLETION-WARRANT-LEAN-INTEGRATION.md) verifies the current ideal-completion and admissibility discussion: seven modules, 228 named theorems and 338 declaration audits, with no custom axioms or proof holes and standard logical dependencies exposed. General infinite-domain results include the unique completion role, finite-refutable admission, finite/infinite history correspondence, powerset completion, finite-branching lifting and genuine global counterexamples. Finite proof and finite observed prefix have different evidential roles. This supplies a precise preservation requirement for candidate norms: retain their complete content, including global conditions and legitimate discharge. It does not establish first ethical warrant or ultimate root uniqueness. The normative first priority remains the practical force of the whole relationship against informed finality; checked preservation supports that investigation. [Ren’s new standard](../../../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-07-lean-proof-requirement.md) applies to this discussion and all new proofs, not a retrospective repository-wide audit.

**Merged unfinished obligations continued, 7 October 2026:** [The integrated round](../../../research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/MERGED-OBLIGATIONS-INTEGRATION.md) connects phase acquired from actual readings to contact inquiry/formation without reset, extends conservation and finite-support bounds through the whole revising process, and places an actual contact profile on the same run. A pair-consuming rival shows formation's interface draw is unnecessary for those support/termination conclusions. The [naturalism continuation](../../../research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/ETHICAL-NATURALISM-ACTUAL-RECOVERY.md) preserves actual interruption despite restoration and rejects reduction to eventual possibilities; warranted actual fulfillment and restored access remain distinct targets. These are verified local connections and a representation correction supporting the first priority. The [period continuation](../../../research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/PERIOD-LOWER-BOUND-ARGUMENT.md) adds a reviewed general reduction with separately checked components; its composed original-game Lean theorem remains open, alongside universal ethical warrant and a derived renewable law.

**Regenerative direction and direct material renewal, 7 October 2026:** [The integrated continuation](../../../research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/REGENERATIVE-DIRECTION-INTEGRATION.md) defends actually formed regenerative capacity as a positive contribution under Ren’s criterion, with a rival that accepts abiotic achievement but disputes the significance of unused productive capacity. A direct Lean proof shows actual inquiry and recovery regenerate a backed formation input, retaining finite source and all eight supported stages; initial formation requires nine. A separate checked distinction prevents complete-law reachability from being mistaken for already formed organization. Fresh verification passes 27 modules/647 audits, with 21 new declarations and explicit standard dependencies. **Scope corrected after Ren’s reply:** the permanently unused-capacity claim was a stronger AI conjecture, not a required inference. The [future-horizon correction](../../../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-07-generativity-future-horizon.md) returns the next study to what actual generative relations can enable over further continuation, preserving uncertainty, support, finite achievements and losses. Neither another organization nor universal ethical warrant is proved.

**Three-obligation continuation, 7 October 2026:** [The completed formal continuation](../../../research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/OPEN-OBLIGATIONS-CONTINUATION.md) proves the original inquiry game's arbitrary-period lower bound and removes the period-eight cutoff from conditional draw selection. A fresh 21-module Lean build passes all 1,183 audits with empty dependencies. The port renewal study gives an exact finite encounter horizon and checked obstructions to the specified contact-only renewal mapping. The attempted inescapability argument supplies no new universal ethical warrant; the first-warrant objective remains open.

**First parallel deep dive integrated, 7 October 2026; scoped round complete.** The [integration](../../../research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/NORMATIVE-WARRANT-INTEGRATION.md) joins the [affirmative argument](../../../research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/NORMATIVE-WARRANT-ARGUMENT.md), [counterreview](../../../research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/NORMATIVE-WARRANT-COUNTERREVIEW.md) and [six primary comparisons](../../../research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/NORMATIVE-WARRANT-SOURCES.md). It defends a narrow inference constraint: blocking uptake does not, by itself, answer preserved adverse grounds or strengthen a verdict's warrant. A positive duty of answerability still needs substantive practical grounds. The informed-finality rival accepts the narrow result, so neither universal corrigibility nor loss of all warrant follows. Existing D3/D6 and F9 are credited; closure, terminal goods and nonreflective lives remain. **Next central connection:** defend or revise the practical force of the whole undertaking's relation to serious impairment, against informed finality and appropriative authority, without assuming answerability as the conclusion. The four-continuation comparison is specified, not an executed experiment. This is philosophical analysis and internal AI review, not a new machine proof or completed universal ethics.

**Ethical naturalism, first round 6 October 2026, reviewed (伦理自然主义):** [The study note](../../../research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/ETHICAL-NATURALISM.md) answers Ren's message that the four arguments for ethical naturalism can be proved. After an independent AI review, the result is stated as formal features of one candidate natural property (whole-history non-loss of realizable activities), not proof of the arguments' premises; no moral predicate is formalized. The quality is not in general fixed by endpoints and action word. Its structural credentials hold for every step relation, so they select neither orientation nor property. Under a stated non-undermining premise, continuation inclusion is the greatest admissible orientation; fixed scopes and complete or scalar rankings fail it over actual transitions. Preserved counterexamples: restraint, every exclusive commitment and every recovered dip register as damage, so the protected scope and a positive measure of generation still have to be derived. [Lean package](../../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/naturalism/README.md): 53 audits, no custom axioms. **Protected-scope continuation (reviewed, negative):** [a scope derived from the process](../../../research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/ETHICAL-NATURALISM-PROTECTED-SCOPE.md) (ongoing activities that remove no other) separates restraint from harm only with every activity in scope and only where the victim removes nothing, and separates commitment from destruction only with the exercised scope; no single scope does both, and unrecovered falls are not damage. Next bridge: compare loss and recovery on the actual whole history at one level. **Under Ren's no-axioms rule:** the package now depends on no axioms at all, and the three remaining inputs (orientation, whole scope, individuation of activities) are proved necessary in `PremiseNecessity.lean`. **Nondual restatement (Ren's correction):** evaluation is modelled as a record inside the same process; the record equals the whole-history relation, so supervenience and identity are derived rather than posed between two kinds, and the orientation question becomes which evaluation relation forms in the history (`EmbeddedEvaluation.lean`, 110 audits, no axioms). **Persistence, from the core alone:** with finitely many states, an evaluating activity persists exactly when a state the process regenerates, where it stays realizable, is reachable, and the return path loses no eventual realizability (`RegeneratingPersistence.lean`; package 120 audits, no axioms). **Enacted orientation (7 October 2026, Ren asked whether the generative direction is already derived): partly.** At the level of eventual realizability, where persistence is defined, the two orientations are not mirror images inside the process: no transition gains, so the loss orientation endorses every transition and distinguishes nothing; transitions that lose can be enacted fewer times than there are states along any history, without decidable equality; and, with decidable state equality, a marking with arbitrarily many finite enactments has some reachable marked transition on a return path preserving eventual realizability. This bounds strictly losing enactments and locates a preserving return; it does not make every marked transition preserving or select an actual infinite history. Preserved: loss can precede persistence (`severing`), finiteness is necessary (`ladder`), and at immediate feasibility loss recurs with recovery (`dip`), so the opposite-orientation counterexample stands there. Persistence is not shown to be good ([§11](../../../research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/ETHICAL-NATURALISM.md#11-评价被实行时取向是否由延续导出), `EnactedOrientation.lean`; package 147 audits, no axioms).

**How valuation develops, first outcome completed 7 October 2026:** [The bounded argument](../../../research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/VALUATION-DEVELOPMENT-ARGUMENT.md) and its [current formulation](../../../../theories/shengsheng-buxi-generative-existence/unified-2026-10-05/VALUATION-DEVELOPMENT.md) answer the planned study below, as Ren asked in the project thread. It proves six results over every organization of a stated selection/retention form, each paired with finite countermodels. (1) Later priority changes only through retained differences; otherwise the change is an environmental trace. (2) Self-history does not fix how others' consequences are valued, independently of the organization's own law. (3) Prediction and scoring trade off and cannot be separated from choices alone. (4) Consequence-driven valuation can be captured by decoupled signals and needs variation to correct. (5) Shared exposure under contraction erases valuation differences, while presentation, multistability, resolution and access can preserve them. (6) The lower capacities form no forced ladder. The [formal companion](../../../../experiments/valuation-development/README.md) has 33 core-Lean 4.29.1 results with no custom axioms. A separate internal AI review accepted it with corrections, which have been applied. Constraint on valuations across continuing histories needs no analyst reward, and it guarantees nothing favorable. The organization boundary, coupling signs and variation remain supplied. No empirical or ethical claim is made. **Closed-loop continuation (same day):** under per-encounter contraction, amplification of valuation differences must pass through a selection difference, and scheduled variation removes it. Witnesses show self-confirming underestimates, and one-unit differences leading either to exhaustion or to sustained specialization. These are 11 further core-Lean results. **Exploration continuation:** in a world whose consequences do not change, coverage rather than its source equalizes estimates. Retained curiosity or optimism can correct self-confirming underestimates, and a low retained estimate can prevent correction (11 further results). **Completed:** changing worlds, noise, interaction, and levels 5–6 (§14, 15 further results; 70 in total). The study's first outcome is complete at its deterministic formal scope. **Bridges closed (§16, 33 further results; 103 in total):** with chance, persistent exploration reaches an option and estimates it exactly with probability at least `1 - (r/q)^n`, while in a noisy witness greedy lock-in tends to certainty. Across generations, ledgers satisfying the prefix bound are closed under succession with inheritance and under division, so a demanding lineage needs arrivals that keep coming late. **On the shared process (§17, 14 results, 117 in total):** the generation ledger is the core support law U1 of a lineage process, and the lineage bound is its prefix bound. The renewal channel process obeys U1, so lineage and the renewal–closure theorem hold on one process, and persistence is decided by population membership, not by each kind's balance. **Valuations decide what is formed (§18, 7 results, 124 in total):** the formation sequence is derived from a valuation in the process. Some valuation keeps a kind forever exactly when a self-renewing population contains it; §18 tabulates every remaining input as derived, proved necessary or quantified over. **One process (§19, 6 results, 130 in total):** organization and world, and one center and another, are cuts of one joint process, and neither cut is privileged. **Axiom-free (7 October):** 118 of 130 results depend on no axiom, not even `propext`/`Quot.sound`; the other 12 inherit them only from shared support and renewal theorems still being rewritten. Empirical identification, material realization and the ethical question are handed on.

**Least period selected by guaranteed drawn activity; formed contact joined to R0, 7 October 2026 (Lean 4.29.1, 43 empty audits):** [The study](../../../research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/PHASE-PERIOD-SELECTION.md) measures what an organization must draw, in the worst case, to stand knowingly at each contact value from an unknown phase and inversion. This is the native funding guard taken over starts it cannot tell apart. Three phases need exactly 7 commands. No period 1–8 manages with 6. Seven is reached only by repetitions of a three-phase pattern, and those are excluded by their retained cells. So for periods 3–8 the three-phase organization draws strictly least (`three_phase_least_guaranteed_draw`). Kept as counterexamples: on a uniform average with known phase, `00101` is cheaper; a single requested value does not select; the lower bounds are bounded to period 8. `FormedContactCycle` proves that formation turns one-valued arrival into the native two-valued pattern (R0) and that two-valued arrival requires formation. The sensor reading used for phase recovery is independent of the contact, so that part is not joined. `ContactFormation.lean` was copied unchanged from the contact thread. With the process-law closure, 36 modules and 720 audits are empty. **Next:** compose reading-based phase recovery with the two-probe contact inquiry; give a general proof of the period lower bound.

**Three-phase cycle made accountable, 6 October 2026 (Lean 4.29.1, 52 empty audits):** [The study](../../../research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/PHASE-CYCLE-DERIVATION.md) takes up the process law's supplied cycle. Cyclicity follows from one finite, distinction-retaining primitive advance. Three is the least period at which a two-valued contact keeps its phase recoverable under unknown reading inversion. Periods 1–2 fail, 4 fails the two-advance reach, and 5 qualifies, so three is not forced. The phase recurs while the whole configuration does not. The retaining advance, finiteness, binary contact, downstream requirements and least-period selection remain supplied. The [new module](../../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/inquiry-root/sufficiency/PhaseCycleDerivation.lean) builds with all 52 audits empty; with the process-law closure, 33 modules and 651 audits are empty. An independent Python enumeration agrees. **Next:** derive the period selection from sustaining capacity and join the contact-formation result.

**Dynamics selection read relationally, 6 October 2026 (UTC):** the static/toggle counterexample to selecting a dynamics from distinctions supplies state identity before relations. [RelationalDynamics](../../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/deeper/RelationalDynamics.lean) identifies states by their relations (bisimulation) and proves, without any axiom, that static and toggle are one dynamics with the same histories, and that every always-continuing single-event process is the one recurrence. What remains distinct is activity versus none. The [dependency map ledger](../../../research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/PROCESS-LAW-ENTRY-DEPENDENCY-MAP.md#7-no-assumptions-ledger) and the [open-items context](../../../research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/PROCESS-LAW-OPEN-ITEMS-CONTEXT.md) record the narrowed item. [RelationalContinuation](../../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/deeper/RelationalContinuation.lean) (7 October) extends it to any events: without supplied event identity, the continuing part of every process is the one recurrence, so a dynamics differs only in where continuation is kept or lost. Event identity remains supplied, with a preserved counterexample; whether it too can be read relationally is the next bridge.

**Native port joined to the conditional laws, 6 October 2026 (UTC):** [the dependency map](../../../research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/PROCESS-LAW-ENTRY-DEPENDENCY-MAP.md) classifies everything the process-law Lean entry relies on: derived, universal over a class, needing evidence, or supplied here. Its first open interface is now closed. [NativePortContinuation](../../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/conditional/native-port/README.md) proves that every native call conserves what is held (存) plus what is enacted (化) relative to what has arrived (来). The port therefore satisfies U1 with equality and inherits U2. With only its own source, it has no infinite execution. [Ren's naming direction](../../../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-07-market-names-and-continuation.md) replaces the fuel/spent/income reading. At his follow-up direction the Lean names themselves were changed across the U1 package and the native port (`SustainLaw`, held/enacted/arrived, `draw`, `support`); the [rename record](../../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/verification-history/2026-10-06-before-vocabulary-rename/README.md) preserves the old sources and checks that all 971 audits across 49 modules are unchanged under the renames. The map now carries a [no-assumptions ledger](../../../research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/PROCESS-LAW-ENTRY-DEPENDENCY-MAP.md#7-no-assumptions-ledger) that marks every input derived, proved necessary or still assumed, following [Ren's standing rule](../../../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-06-no-assumptions-no-axioms-rule.md). Under that rule the conditional package and the native-port module were re-proved without `propext` and `Quot.sound` (all 90 audits now empty), and [CalibratedMacro](../../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/inquiry-root/sufficiency/CalibratedMacro.lean) removes the receipt-calibration hypothesis from the macro probe law by using the inversion the port's own inquiry acquires. The fresh build has 23 new audits, all within `propext`/`Quot.sound`. The next bridge stays contact formation leading to a changed effective law, which is in progress in a separate thread.

**Contact-formation track closed, 6 October 2026:** [the Lean study](../../../research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/CONTACT-FORMATION-LAW.md) removes the contact coordinate's status as a fixed input of the [process-law derivation](../../../research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/PROCESS-LAW-DERIVATION.md). A general theorem shows that any coordinate retained by a process can change only through formation events. Formation draws on pairs recovered from the port's own recorded failed transfers. A two-probe inquiry the port runs on itself soundly decides when to form, acquires receipt calibration from the port's own transfer record, and reveals the inversion once the contact is open; a blocked contact hides the inversion and receipts alone confound the two inversions. One executed history goes from a blocked port with no spares through inquiry, recovery and formation to an actual transfer, and ends holding far more reserve than the same inquiries without revision. The formation disposition instantiates the valuation-development universals with a consequence law derived from native execution, and every valuation organization embeds in the core with its runs as traces. 119 new audits, 718 in the 39-module closure, all empty. **Revising port, 6 October:** the reading and decision are now built into the port, so the valuation organization and the extended port are one process. Its retained readings are exactly the organization's run, its histories are port histories, and the class changes only through its own selection. The decision rule, the two probes and the intact reset are each proved necessary, as is consuming a recovered pair at formation (61 audits; 779 in 40 modules, all empty). The activity/formation split is derived as a relation of each step to a coordinate, for every process (9 audits). **Unified entry, 7 October:** [the connection table](../../../research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/UNIFIED-ENTRY.md) records which packages apply to this one process. The alignment package now reads on the port's own response table, with two mismatches proved: the port has three tables, not four, and the decision to form is not a one-entry continuation (13 audits; 801 in 46 modules, all empty). U1 for the whole port waits on the rename; renewal and naturalism are not yet connected. Still to derive: the recovery rate, formation's interface demand, and formation as a rearrangement of the port's own material rather than a primitive event.

**Renewal and closure of constraints — roadmap, 6 October 2026:** Requested by Ren; status: **in progress (Phases 0–2 done)**. Ren set the goal of a formal theory of renewal with one nontrivial theorem characterizing when a process renews its own provision instead of importing it, stated against Montévil and Mossio's closure of constraints. [The roadmap](../../../research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/RENEWAL-CLOSURE-ROADMAP.md) proposes a renewal–closure theorem. Under finite kinds, finite spans, positive formation demand and arrival only through live constraints, renewal without imported organization is possible exactly when the dependence graph has a cycle and a renewal inequality (`a·ℓ ≥ m`) holds as specified; otherwise there is an explicit deadline. Consequences: closure plus openness is not sufficient, and M&M's timescale separation becomes a quantitative condition. The roadmap builds on U1/U2, `SupportNecessity`, `NativePortContinuation` and `FormationClosure`. It has phases for prior art, constructive necessity, sufficiency, generalization, derivation of the constraint notion inside the core, and comparators. [Preliminary bounded-horizon checks](../../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/renewal-closure/preliminary/README.md) guard the conventions only. Nothing is proved yet. It is aimed at Ren's research center: how the whole unfolding generates or damages the conditions of its later realization, renewal and correction. Phase 2b defines renewability of whole configurations, shows goal projections cannot capture it (the NONINSTRUMENTAL-CONCERN §15 counterexample), and covers the conditions of correction. Renewability is one ingredient of the whole appraisal, not the appraisal. **Phases 0–1 done, 6 October:** [results](../../../research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/RENEWAL-CLOSURE-PHASE1.md). In Lean (32 audits; propext/Quot.sound only), late activity descends from a realized closure; acyclic support has a deadline without a supplied rank, which closes the gap SupportNecessity named; and constraints that bring in less than they demand end whatever their closure, so closure plus openness is not sufficient. Prior art: the cycle half parallels Unterberger–Nghe's strongly-connected-component condition; no exact threshold under finite constraint lifetimes was found. **Phase 2 done, 6 October:** [the renewal–closure theorem](../../../research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/RENEWAL-CLOSURE-PHASE2.md) is proved in Lean (95 audits; propext/Quot.sound only). With finitely many kinds and bounded spans, a law admits endless renewal without imported organization exactly when some nonempty population of kinds, closed under support by constraints that outlast a tick, opens over its spans at least what forming it demands. Otherwise every history ends by an explicit deadline computed from the initial reserve and tokens. Timescale separation comes out of the accounting. A worked example shows the kind that captures the flow can lie outside every two-sided closure of the support relation. **Phase 2b, first result:** [correction capacity](../../../research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/RENEWAL-CLOSURE-PHASE2B.md) can be kept forever exactly when some self-renewing population contains it (111 audits). A closure can renew forever while its correction capacity lapses by an explicit time. Whole configurations (135 audits): renewability is never gained along a history without import, only kept or lost; a seeded configuration reaches a standing renewal; no goal projection decides renewability. Under Ren's no-assumption rule (146 audits): the span and rate bounds are now read off the list of kinds, and the positive-span and initial-tokens-within-span premises are removed, so the theorems hold for histories from any start. Decidable equality of kinds is removed, and finitely many kinds and positive demand (for the deadline read from the start) are proved necessary by counterexamples (167 audits). **7 October:** the package uses no axiom at all; `propext` and `Quot.sound` are removed and all 219 audits are empty. Next: Phase 4, deriving the constraint–flow law from the generative core ([handoff](../../../handoffs/2026-10-07-renewal-closure.md)).

**Explore before 10 October 2026 — how valuation develops:** Requested by Ren on 7 October in the side conversation; status: **first outcome completed 7 October** (entry above); original plan preserved below. Investigate universal conditional constraints on the formation and revision of operational valuation within the [generative-grounding programme](../../../../theories/shengsheng-buxi-generative-existence/unified-2026-10-05/GENERATIVE-GROUNDING.md). Starting questions: how differentiated dependencies acquire selective responsiveness; how encounters leave effects that change later priorities; how anticipation and revision of those priorities become possible. Test causal-access and retention requirements, the distinction between accurate prediction and valuation, and why connected systems may develop different values. Treat the proposed progression as a candidate requiring mechanisms, not an inevitable sequence. Trace the formation of evaluative mechanisms instead of silently supplying a reward function. Keep functional valuation, conscious experience and ethical justification distinct, with participants and their relationships developing within one unfolding. Intended first outcome: a bounded argument identifying conditional universals, supplied premises, counterexamples and open empirical questions. Universal ethical necessity remains a separate unresolved question.

**Value question and descriptive dynamics, 7 October 2026:** [Ren identifies the limitation](../../../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-07-value-question-and-dynamics.md) of pursuing a normative answer through descriptions of dynamics. [The canonical correction](../../../../theories/shengsheng-buxi-generative-existence/unified-2026-10-05/UNIFIED-ETHICAL-GROUNDING.md#describing-dynamics-and-answering-the-value-question) distinguishes dynamical explanation, conditional ethical application and normative justification. The performance test remains a conditional application; it does not derive its ethical priority. More comprehensive description alone is no longer treated as the missing value warrant. Universal grounding and the necessity of an extra ethical principle both remain unsettled.

**A successful pursuit can warrant revision, 7 October 2026:** [The executed comparison](../../../research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/SITUATED-ETHICAL-DISCERNMENT.md#continuation-test-changing-a-successful-pursuit) preserves a meaningful performance that genuinely improves, while completing it entails serious sustained impairment of ordinary living. The candidate defends legitimate cancellation and recovery despite losing the shared completion. This extends a bounded priority argument; a stable wider concern explains the concrete goal change. Supplied understanding is not a demonstration of learning, and first ethical grounding remains open.

**Self-good and practical reasons clarified, 7 October 2026:** [The consolidation](../../../research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/WHOLE-PROCESS-GROUNDING-ARGUMENT.md#34-self-good-practical-reasons-and-the-remaining-ground) makes the candidate’s good-to-reason principle explicit. Under it, self-care can favor action without an outward payoff; permission, obligation and authority require further grounds. Noninstrumental good can still have a constitutive explanation. This develops the practical articulation of self-inclusion and connects to situated discernment; review found no new proof of first goodness. The unified foundation remains unfinished.

**体道与应境中的善，7 October 2026:** [Ren's proposal is preserved](../../../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-07-situated-goodness-and-tidao.md) and [integrated into the ethical theory](../../../../theories/shengsheng-buxi-generative-existence/unified-2026-10-05/UNIFIED-ETHICAL-GROUNDING.md#situated-participation-and-ethical-discernment). The [supporting study](../../../research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/SITUATED-ETHICAL-DISCERNMENT.md) distinguishes disclosure, realization and cultivation within one history: participation can form the relationship being understood, while prior potential itself has a formative history. Bounded Wang Yangming, Zhang Zai and Zhuangzi readings inform the candidate. Stable ultimate orientation remains compatible; fluency, agreement and adaptation do not establish ethical wisdom. Next test warranted change of concrete pursuits under actual consequences. This is conceptual integration, not completed universal grounding.

**Future fields and formed claims, 7 October 2026:** [The continuation](../../../research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/FUTURE-GOOD-INTEGRAL.md#a-future-field-can-generate-differentiated-claims) shows how a warranted selection can form a particular delivery claim without reducing the loser's basic standing. Honoring and reversing the draw have identical initial recipient distributions, joint entropy and mutual information; the actual undertaking distinguishes them. This conditional result refines field representation and permits justified closure. The next bridge is the undertaking's ethical authority, which neither symmetry nor performance establishes.

**Fair chances within the future field, 7 October 2026:** [The new positive test](../../../research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/FUTURE-GOOD-INTEGRAL.md#fair-chances-as-a-quality-of-the-organized-future) investigates actual organization of access as a constituent of fair treatment. Equal probabilities follow conditionally from a substantive neutrality requirement; endpoint-only expected values cannot express the proposed procedural difference. A permissible-tie-break rival remains substantive. The result strengthens the field candidate without deriving equal standing, universal lottery authority or ethical necessity. Chance and receipt remain distinct, and the full grounding task continues.

**Renewal and practical importance tested, 7 October 2026:** [The study](../../../research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/WHOLE-PROCESS-GROUNDING-ARGUMENT.md#33-renewal-causal-importance-and-the-good-realized) develops this connection. A conditional extension of the injury/repair comparison and the inherited terminal-good case challenge recurrence as the ethical criterion; no indefinite physical cycle is demonstrated. A working transfer of support changes one practical reason’s basis while the former maintainer’s own good remains included. First ethical warrant and contested allocation rules remain open.

**Future fields and causal organization, 7 October 2026:** [Ren's authorized integration](../../../research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/FUTURE-GOOD-INTEGRAL.md#future-fields-and-changes-in-causal-organization) connects changes in the conditions generating futures to prospective ethical significance. **Actual-relation test adjudicated, 7 October 2026:** [The adjudication](../../../research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/WHOLE-PROCESS-GROUNDING-ARGUMENT.md#adjudication-of-the-proposed-discriminator) refines the current inquiry. **Whole appraisal without an additional beneficiary, 7 October 2026:** [The continuation](../../../research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/WHOLE-PROCESS-GROUNDING-ARGUMENT.md#32-whole-goodness-needs-no-additional-beneficiary) develops this connection. **Mechanism-status correction, 7 October 2026:** [the repository reconciliation](../../../research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/MECHANISM-STATUS-RECONCILIATION.md) confirms Ren’s observation that several repeatedly named unfinished capacities already exist. Contact formation, contextual recording, tests of experimental access, effective-law derivation, architecture and generator formation, acquired decoding, inquiry selection/synthesis and finite supported succession have accepted results at their stated scopes. This is status reconciliation, with no new mechanism or scientific rerun.

**Self-good within the whole, 7 October 2026:** [Ren clarifies](../../../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-07-self-good-within-the-whole.md) that genuine good done to oneself belongs within the whole. The core is not an earlier owner transferring value to branches. Next test what makes the achieved organization fitting and which prescriptions its explanation warrants, retaining the whole as the grounding unit.

**整体积分与局部启动的边界，2026年10月6日：** [Ren 的纠正](../../../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-06-yiti-ren-coformation-completion.md#整体积分的纠正)将本轮中心放回整个生成过程及其未来，不能以保护某项预期或加总既定目的替代。[仁之树的更新](../../../../theories/shengsheng-buxi-generative-existence/unified-2026-10-05/NONINSTRUMENTAL-CONCERN.md#15-整体积分与局部启动的边界)保留新的材料连接：实际形成／完成的通道被取得并改变后来启动；九条运行的2412项扩展事件通过独立重构，30项新增 Lean 声明审计通过。保护乙成功仍可留下自身永无继续路径的新甲；这一后继封闭及相同局部成功留下不同新结构的实例均已形式化，局部成功仍不充分代表整体。[整合与审查](../../../research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/YITI-REN-INITIATION-INTEGRATION.md)接回已有整体未来判据与条件积分表达，并保留局部规律及普遍证成边界。**下一中心连接：**整个后续关系的改变怎样进入理解、目的和评价范围本身的形成与纠正，以及这种转变何以构成整体的生成或退化。

The [integral continuation](../../../research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/FUTURE-GOOD-INTEGRAL.md#the-future-of-the-whole-as-the-criterion) identifies a concrete comparison between local success that closes shared renewal/correction and transformation that restores them, preserving losses and actual realizations. Existing material continuation evidence supplies a structural interface, not ethical proof. Earlier C1/C2 and the newly read grief debate remain supporting branches, not the foundation's mandatory first ground. **Next:** establish the whole-quality significance of specified changes in shared future conditions. Universal necessity remains open.

Two primary comparisons constrain direct-warrant and evolutionary-explanation claims; the original Japanese paper is preserved with provenance. Its challenge to explanatory privilege does not establish the defeat of all direct warrant, and its adaptive scenario remains unverified. **Next:** assess whether fitting grief or remorse eliminates a local adverse contribution or realizes additional good alongside it. Universal necessity and practical scope remain open.

**活动范围的形成、取得与结束，2026年10月6日：** [仁之树内连接的新一轮](../../../../theories/shengsheng-buxi-generative-existence/unified-2026-10-05/NONINSTRUMENTAL-CONCERN.md#14-活动及其范围本身的形成改变和结束)把直接给定的继续范围改为实际占材活动、阶段接触及读者使用。完成与解离不同；甲结束不抹掉乙、既有产品或发生史。九条新运行通过独立重放，26项新增 Lean 声明通过；本律中共同支持耗尽后、仍处中间阶段的乙不可完成获全有限历史证明。两条单列的 Lean 历史又证明取得的空范围不保证所有原产品已形成。[整合](../../../research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/YITI-REN-SCOPE-INTEGRATION.md)连接正当结束论证及真实帮助情境的识别；经验设计尚未实施。**下一连接：**启动、停止、优先的取得和修订。反应/接触配方、当前可行性反馈与调度仍输入；生活对应及无假设普遍根据未完成。

Strict identification, recording and completed correct reading occur with probability 9/64 conditional on the new controlled construction. The whole 235-unit account retains actor four, recipient twenty and reader six. Archive shortage can leave the original actions unchanged; reader shortage can preserve complete records while preventing a completed factual result. Outage zero remains unknown. Fixed recipes, faithful acquisition, stable target and context integrity remain premises; corrupt tags supply an explicit failure. The next join consumes these correlated orientation facts in paid rooted reasoning and effective action under justified relevance and root premises. The former raw-Bool assay is not inherited, and this finite bridge does not complete the foundation.

**Start with the [current goal and generative grounding](../../../../theories/shengsheng-buxi-generative-existence/unified-2026-10-05/GENERATIVE-GROUNDING.md), then the [unified foundation](../../../../theories/shengsheng-buxi-generative-existence/unified-2026-10-05/START-HERE.md).** The [formation of concern](../../../research/2026-10-05-concern-theory/START-HERE.md), [revisable concern](../../../research/2026-10-05-revisable-concern/START-HERE.md), and [integrated concern settlement](../../../../theories/shengsheng-buxi-generative-existence/unified-2026-10-05/REVISABLE-CONCERN-SETTLEMENT.md) are contributing studies. These are repository files, not exported copies.

## The central research line

**Local architecture formation, 6 October 2026:** [the constructed bridge](../../../../theories/shengsheng-buxi-generative-existence/unified-2026-10-05/ARCHITECTURE-FORMATION.md) now follows the actual formed learner into paid receptor/comparator fabrication, local bonding, target-specific use, finite retention and a second assembly changed by consequence-funded guides. The well-funded direct-path probability becomes 2/3 after a response-guide copy, stays 1/2 after a matched inert copy, and becomes 1/3 after a filter-guide copy. Fifty declared comparison arms retain initial failures, costly intermediates, harmful transfer and exact conventional equivalence. Primary live copying increases recipient use without increasing total graph retention; costly maintenance can favor filtering. The [review](../../../research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/ARCHITECTURE-FORMATION-REVIEW.md) accepts the finite conditional mechanism. Component contracts, scheduling and target calibration remain supplied. The following accepted continuation now joins locally formed routing to acquired recipient-specific evidence and correction without concluding that concern must emerge.

**Architecture-to-recipient learning, 6 October 2026:** [the completed join](../../../../theories/shengsheng-buxi-generative-existence/unified-2026-10-05/ARCHITECTURE-LEARNING.md) carries actual formed graphs, inherited records, spare parts and remaining actor fuel into paid recipient evidence, a changed response, subsequent positive qualification and final maintenance. The declared initial bank leaves the parent process unchanged. Seventy-two case/arm laws pass independent checks; the strict primary history has unconditional probability 11/405. Its intersection with earlier strict motor-coupling revision is zero under these resources. Faithful feedback improves the recipient outcome but gives no private retention advantage over matched absent feedback; outages, misleading channels, aliased writes and stale qualification preserve failures. The [review](../../../research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/ARCHITECTURE-LEARNING-REVIEW.md) accepts this finite conditional bridge. Acquisition recipes, calibration and scheduling remain supplied. The next central connection is formed understanding → justified evaluative influence → effective corrigible action through succession; the separate [fresh-evidence/reason-revision join](../../../../theories/shengsheng-buxi-generative-existence/unified-2026-10-05/RECIPIENT-REASON-REVISION.md) remains accepted at its own scope.

**Evaluative application, 6 October 2026:** [the completed conditional synthesis](../../../../theories/shengsheng-buxi-generative-existence/unified-2026-10-05/EVALUATIVE-APPLICATION.md#completing-the-application-when-the-protocol-obstructs-repair) now settles the outstanding source-bound acquisition/repair application. At the actual continuing count-four state, retained occupancy identifies the completed four-unit depletion; every service-starting continuation still requires two additional teaching payments. The licensed no-further-payment and service-starting sets are therefore disjoint under an independently warranted prohibition. Legitimate suspension can be justified while repair remains outstanding; independently authorized, necessary and proportionate inquiry can instead remain warranted, and unknown authority stays unresolved. This is a definite application and feasibility result, not learner-selected restraint or installed early repair. [Contract §13](../../../research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/EVALUATIVE-APPLICATION-CONTRACT.md#13-evidence-can-warrant-stopping-a-protocol-that-withholds-repair), [math §14](../../../research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/EVALUATIVE-APPLICATION-MATH.md#14-joint-feasibility-of-restraint-and-repair-at-the-actual-state) and [review §23](../../../research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/EVALUATIVE-APPLICATION-REVIEW.md#23-bounded-completion-of-the-obstructed-repair-application) retain gross/net distinctions, actual control powers, costs and independent grounds. Existing joint formation and revisable concern already answer selected costly correction and renewal; acquired influence, repair, succession and generic correction keep their positive scopes and distinct histories. Earlier uncertainty, delivery-provenance and fixed-assessment results remain intact. This completes this branch's conditional explanatory/application integration, adds no controller or scientific run, and leaves the stronger universal/no-assumptions claim unproved.

**External cues → recipient-directed action, 6 October 2026:** the [new empirical audit](../../../../theories/shengsheng-buxi-generative-existence/unified-2026-10-05/RECIPIENT-CUE-GROUNDING.md) reanalyzes a verified public preview of a published rat-provisioning experiment. All 24 available pairs yield a mean difference of +1.333 pulls per session; all 24 single-animal omissions remain positive. Separate implementation and reconstruction agree on 48 rows, missingness, paired bootstrap and 50 exact assumption-conditional reference calculations. Original CSV bytes were not retrieved, the published n=23 discrepancy is unexplained, and the local plan followed knowledge of published/preview outcomes. A precise observational-equivalence argument shows why this contrast cannot identify recipient-specific representation or moral concern. This closes a bounded retrospective empirical checkpoint, not the full cognition-to-concern derivation. **Next connection:** independently acquired source information → preserved beneficiary identity → effective delivery under mismatch and change, with cue-response/activity alternatives and actual recipient consequences distinguished. [Methods and review](../../../research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/CUE-GROUNDING-REVIEW.md) preserve the sample, timing and interpretation limits.

**Acquired evidence now constrains reason calibration, 6 October 2026:** the [supporting interface](../../../../theories/shengsheng-buxi-generative-existence/unified-2026-10-05/SEMANTIC-CALIBRATION.md) uses the actual retained recipient command and qualification flag to expose the existing archive-tag fault. Twelve new Lean audits derive the stable-law invariant from the original updater; separate reconstruction checks 666 rows, 1,850 acquired transitions and 10,656 inspection scenarios. Ordinary comparison matches exactly. The new check-before-import result shows that copying a reason into the controller before checking produces circular agreement, including for actual corrupt-source publications. Common corruption and silent change remain failures; correct new evidence may disagree with stale memory. This closes a conditional information interface, with physical reading, reconciliation, objective justification and authority still required. **Next connection:** pay for correctly bound pre-import evidence access or separately backed acquired memory in the actual command-use continuation, preserving recipient effects and revision rather than adding more abstract agreement checks. [Review and preserved correction](../../../research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/SEMANTIC-CALIBRATION-REVIEW.md).
