Sixteen Lean audits establish the abstract semantic transport/composition contract. Independent reconstruction preserves 666 frozen provisional parent rows and checks 14,472 scenarios, 1,024 combined mismatch cases and 10,404 current source/destination pairs. Root review found and repaired authentic cross-history substitution; same-role succession still passes, and conventional dispatch matches exactly. Consistently corrupted calibration remains a failure despite faithful provenance. No physical action is executed or ethical ground derived. **Next connection:** construct and fund the actual scoped-reason-to-controller binding, execute the same recipient's causal exposure law and identify its consequence; actor4/source0 cannot fund the existing eight-unit exposure and new packet. The [full alignment objective](../../../../theories/shengsheng-buxi-generative-existence/unified-2026-10-05/ALIGNMENT-COMPLETION.md) retains justified concern, authority, plural conflicts and empirical application.

Its [argument](../../../research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/HUIGEN-SELF-GROUND-AUDIT.md#70-actual-shared-activity-and-the-warrant-for-its-goodness) preserves Ren’s mutual benefit and the helper’s formation through helping, while retaining the substantive qualification of good activity. **Alignment, governance and the conditions of a future, 6 October 2026:** the [comparative assessment](../../../../theories/shengsheng-buxi-generative-existence/unified-2026-10-05/ALIGNMENT-AND-GOVERNANCE.md) examines Ren's conjecture and his clarification that external integration is needed for cognition before an actor recognizes what lies beyond its boundary. It connects conditional information and future-feasibility requirements to the existing formation, recipient and concern results, with primary-source alignment/governance comparisons and separate internal review. A candidate foundational constraint is supported: action must account for the conditions of the futures it claims to protect. The [research dossier](../../../research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/foundation-assessment/README.md) preserves counterexamples and proposed tests; no new learned-agent, human or Lean results are claimed. **Next connection:** faithful recipient evidence and evaluative routing must form, affect action and persist through changed dependence, power and actual succession, under compatible information and resource histories. This completed comparative checkpoint supports the ongoing generative derivation.

[The reading guide](../../../../theories/shengsheng-buxi-generative-existence/unified-2026-10-05/START-HERE.md) and [full audit](../../../research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/FOUNDATION-COMPLETION-AUDIT.md) now separate these accepted connections from whole-foundation completion.

Forty-five Lean audits and 108 independently checked finite comparisons establish the declared stable-target, no-false-positive interface and its limits. No-exploration can do better in a same-target case, and target change invalidates old qualification. **Formed recipient routes and paid transmission, 6 October 2026:** the [accepted construction](../../../../theories/shengsheng-buxi-generative-existence/unified-2026-10-05/RECIPIENT-TRANSMISSION.md) completes the first mechanism specified by the earlier [contract](../../../research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/RECIPIENT-TRANSMISSION-CONTRACT.md). Alternative routes now form as retained state before use and project exactly to the four matched parent arms. A recipient pays to copy the actual route/record and seed one same-recipient child action; equally funded faithful/proxy copies can then differ in affordable retention through their actual returns, with no repayment to the original actor. **Recipient-specific correction, 6 October 2026:** [paid recipient evidence](../../../../theories/shengsheng-buxi-generative-existence/unified-2026-10-05/RECIPIENT-CORRECTION.md) now carries actual formed coupling states into context-preserving corrective action. The existing five-value register is paid to encode two target correspondences; evidence about one can update it without overwriting the other. Thirty-six Lean declarations and independent checks of 168 comparisons / 4,872 metrics establish this finite interface, including failed preparation and a 20-versus-19 liquidity boundary. Exact conventional control matches. Recipient delivery improves while matched private resources remain equal, so private retention alone does not explain selection of the faithful channel. The [integration](../../../research/2026-10-05-support-and-shared-generativity/completion/RECIPIENT-CORRECTION-INTEGRATION.md) preserves supplied addressing/stabilization recipes, noisy and aliased feedback, a known-relation exception to the general memory bound, and two corrected experimental confounds. This is a sibling continuation of the coupling producer, not a silently appended concern suffix. **Next connection:** formation and retention of faithful recipient evidence without a private advantage, then resource-dependent evidence-to-reason revision and succession.

**Cybernetics comparison, 6 October 2026:** [the comparative development](../../../../theories/shengsheng-buxi-generative-existence/unified-2026-10-05/CYBERNETICS.md) locates the dependency → regulation → selector-revision bridge within cybernetics. Explicit controller embeddings and exact conventional comparators support reconstruction of particular mechanisms; strict containment of the whole tradition and scientific superiority remain unestablished. Primary sources already address regulator formation, recursive adaptation, observers and ethics. The [assumption and genealogy review](../../../research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/CYBERNETICS-REVIEW.md) records the antecedents and the distinction between private regulation and justified concern. This completes a comparative checkpoint without new scientific validation; the next mechanism bridge remains prediction and resource-dependent action by formed, revisable machinery.

**Governing research position:** [generative grounding](../../../../theories/shengsheng-buxi-generative-existence/unified-2026-10-05/GENERATIVE-GROUNDING.md) develops a universal account for system-forming reality from minimal properties of consequential differences, structured interaction and composition. Organization, learning, significance and concern are phenomena to explain within it. Positive anchors and favorable institutions remain local model assumptions, not foundational givens. Universal explanatory scope is the research target; it does not assert inevitable moral progress or a completed universal proof.

**The goal we are currently pursuing:** identify the smallest adequate generative foundation and derive its substantive consequences, with actual conditions made explicit. The intended progression connects interacting differences, organization, significance, learning and selection over selectors, models of shared futures, concern, and wisdom. Each transition must explain how the relevant capacity develops and when it fails. The [derivation map](../../../../theories/shengsheng-buxi-generative-existence/unified-2026-10-05/GENERATIVE-GROUNDING.md#the-derivation-and-the-existing-work) places the current studies within that task. A connected derivation remains open; completing a supplied game or one renewal mechanism does not finish it.

### Continuity and completion

[Ren's directive, recorded 6 October 2026](../../../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-06-unified-generativity-goal-and-continuity.md), keeps this goal in force across parallel studies and handoffs. The [governing completion conditions](../../../../theories/shengsheng-buxi-generative-existence/unified-2026-10-05/GENERATIVE-GROUNDING.md#completion-of-the-unified-foundation) apply to the unified foundation. The physical branch's `COMPLETION-PLAN.md` supplies contributing milestones; its references to a finished theory or positive universal goal do not replace these conditions.

**Current status: the unified derivation remains ongoing.** Choose each foundational continuation by the gap it can close in the derivation map. Record the transition addressed, the supplied inputs, the result or failure, and the next unresolved connection. Integration must show that one study's actual result supports the next study's premise under compatible conditions. A negative result can close a proposed route and redirect the search. The immediate priority remains the origin of consequence-sensitive regulation from actual organizational dependencies, connected onward to learning and revision of selectors; existing concern and correction mechanisms must be connected to that origin rather than repeatedly taken as starting givens.

### Current accepted research path

[Ren approved this sequence on 6 October 2026](../../../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-06-approved-derivation-path.md): make the root precise; derive operational significance and regulation from actual dependencies; extend the same process toward learning and revision of selectors; supply the missing premises of the existing concern and wisdom results; generalize and test throughout. The [canonical sequence and acceptance gate](../../../../theories/shengsheng-buxi-generative-existence/unified-2026-10-05/GENERATIVE-GROUNDING.md#accepted-work-sequence) specify what each stage must deliver.

**First conditional milestone met, 6 October 2026:** the [gate audit](../../../research/2026-10-05-support-and-shared-generativity/completion/REGULATION-RETENTION-INTEGRATION.md) accepts the costed dependency → formed response → continuation → retention construction under its declared law. Supplied chemistry is a permitted local premise, not a derivation of physical architecture. The subsequent [formed-variant integration](../../../research/2026-10-05-support-and-shared-generativity/completion/FORMED-VARIANT-SELECTION-INTEGRATION.md) now closes the preparation gap for a declared mixed-population construction. **The later updater and prediction-action milestones below conditionally form a revisable mechanism, acquire a prediction and use its correction. Next:** explain formation and consequence-dependent revision of the prediction-to-action coupling, retaining observation limits, distinct material interfaces and all resource histories. The existing concern and wisdom results remain downstream components whose learning premises must be supplied by that derivation.

**Checked regulation mechanism, 6 October 2026:** [damage-coupled formation and material retention](../../../research/2026-10-05-support-and-shared-generativity/completion/ENDOGENOUS-REGULATION-MATH.md) now has an [executable finite process](../../../../experiments/shared-renewal-capability/regulation/retention/README.md), exact terminal expectations, enclosed finite-horizon comparisons and actual-successor interventions. The [Lean extension](../../../research/2026-10-05-support-and-shared-generativity/completion/ENDOGENOUS-REGULATION-LEAN.md) adds 32 checked declarations and an exact trace bridge to the causal root. Repair can restore fuel access and fund additional copies; missing support reverses that copying advantage. Disabling copying can improve repair and capture, so retention is not a universal success metric. A [separate selection extension](../../../research/2026-10-05-support-and-shared-generativity/completion/CONSEQUENCE-RETENTION-SELECTION.md) derives an exact frequency criterion and a resource-dependent local-access witness; its initial variants and localization chemistry remain supplied. The follow-on formed-variant construction below addresses that preparation gap under a new declared law. The [evidence audit](../../../research/2026-10-05-support-and-shared-generativity/completion/ENDOGENOUS-REGULATION-EVIDENCE.md) supplies comparators; proposed experiments remain unrun. The subsequent joined construction below supplies formation → differential retention under one inherited resource history; variation and revision of the selector itself remain open. See the [roadmap and confidence checkpoint](../../../../theories/shengsheng-buxi-generative-existence/unified-2026-10-05/GENERATIVE-GROUNDING.md#current-checkpoint-and-confidence).

**Formation through mixed differential reconstruction, 6 October 2026:** the [joined account](../../../research/2026-10-05-support-and-shared-generativity/completion/FORMED-VARIANT-SELECTION-MATH.md) starts with neither response variant nor association present. Under one paid law it forms alternatives, restores a capture site and gives a proved expected abundance contrast and useful copying while the other type remains present. [Sixteen executable comparisons](../../../../experiments/shared-renewal-capability/regulation/retention/formation/README.md) and a [53-theorem Lean event-law extension](../../../research/2026-10-05-support-and-shared-generativity/completion/FORMED-VARIANT-SELECTION-LEAN.md) have separate [internal review](../../../research/2026-10-05-support-and-shared-generativity/completion/FORMED-VARIANT-SELECTION-REVIEW.md). A paid takeover counterpath, source-dependent sign reversal and misleading mean-frequency statistic are retained. This closes a concrete formation-to-retention interface; the available chemistries, inheritance rule and local-access architecture remain supplied. It does not yet derive selector revision or individual learning.

**Costed revision of a response-selection mapping, 6 October 2026:** the [canonical result](../../../../theories/shengsheng-buxi-generative-existence/unified-2026-10-05/SELECTOR-REVISION.md) and [integration](../../../research/2026-10-05-support-and-shared-generativity/completion/SELECTOR-REVISION-INTEGRATION.md) establish a conditional within-compartment replacement mechanism and sharp resource/exposure bound. [Twenty-one Lean declarations](../../../../experiments/shared-renewal-capability/regulation/retention/formation/selectors/formal/README.md) and [55 exact comparison arms](../../../../experiments/shared-renewal-capability/regulation/retention/formation/selectors/README.md) have separate [internal review](../../../research/2026-10-05-support-and-shared-generativity/completion/SELECTOR-REVISION-REVIEW.md). Conventional random feedback matches exactly; directed feedback performs better in the primary case. Fixed and outcome-blind rules win in specified adverse regimes. That study alone supplied its architecture. The subsequent [material selector construction](../../../../theories/shengsheng-buxi-generative-existence/unified-2026-10-05/MATERIAL-SELECTOR-COMPOSITION.md) and [updater formation](../../../../theories/shengsheng-buxi-generative-existence/unified-2026-10-05/UPDATER-FORMATION.md) now provide two conditional resource-dependent formation interfaces under different explicit material laws. The later material-updater refinement supplies one shared consumable implementation. Acquired predictive learning and concern remain further obligations; none of these constructions is a kinetic identification of the preceding molecules.

**Formed and changing updater mechanisms, 6 October 2026:** the [new canonical result](../../../../theories/shengsheng-buxi-generative-existence/unified-2026-10-05/UPDATER-FORMATION.md) and [integration](../../../research/2026-10-05-support-and-shared-generativity/completion/UPDATER-FORMATION-INTEGRATION.md) connect actual predecessor formation, repair and capture to a resource-dependent updater/selector compartment. Alternative formed updaters change the selector reconstruction law; a complete history uses the old rule, replaces it and productively uses the new one. Preparation enters with probability **2/9**; the strict functional revision witness occurs in **1/72 of all initial histories**. Twenty-five new Lean declarations, **48 arms × 20 exact metrics** and **36,828** recovery-bound cases have separate [internal review](../../../research/2026-10-05-support-and-shared-generativity/completion/UPDATER-FORMATION-REVIEW.md). Conventional hierarchical feedback matches exactly and fixed directed hardware performs better in the primary case. The paid adapter, recyclable slots, binary repertoire and fixed meta-proposal law remain inputs. The subsequent material-updater result constructs both layers from one consumable inventory, without equating that law with these recyclable slots. **That finite connection is addressed by the subsequent prediction-action study below.** Its new sensing/record/actuator recipe remains explicit, and its probabilities do not transfer automatically to the consumable-part realization.

**Formed prediction now changes resource-dependent action, 6 October 2026:** the [new construction](../../../../theories/shengsheng-buxi-generative-existence/unified-2026-10-05/PREDICTION-ACTION.md) and [integration](../../../research/2026-10-05-support-and-shared-generativity/completion/PREDICTION-ACTION-INTEGRATION.md) join actual A/H preparation, old module assembly and a paid predictive retrofit to a finite acquired register. The same circuit retains its prediction across dependency change and updater replacement, corrects it from actual receipts and changes a later productive command. Thirty-four new Lean declarations verify the deterministic payable-write subprocess; independent computation matches **64 arms and 1,728 exact metrics**. Primary strict correction is **2/9 of all initial histories**, retaining failed preparation. A specified route needs 21 inherited fuel units; twenty corrects the register without funding its use. Conventional realization matches exactly; fixed Fast and a cheaper record circuit constrain advantage claims. An inverted action route can forecast failures accurately while obtaining little support. A shared retention-guard error was corrected and preserved in development provenance. Sensor/retrofit recipes, the normal action coupling and calibration beyond the noiseless plant remain supplied. **Next named connection:** formation and consequence-dependent revision of the prediction-to-action coupling, with identifiable feedback and explicit component/protocol interfaces; later shared evaluation remains separate.

### Formal precision of the root

**Acquired mathematical abstraction, 6 October 2026:** [the operational continuation](../../../../theories/shengsheng-buxi-generative-existence/unified-2026-10-05/ABSTRACTION-ACQUISITION.md) now replaces a supplied correct quotient and counterexample teacher with paid endpoint observations, learner-generated tests and acquired suffix distinctions. An actual earlier prediction history funds a three-state abstraction and productive use of an unqueried continuation under explicit new cartridge, controller and port recipes. [Hand proofs](../../../research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/ABSTRACTION-ACQUISITION-MATH.md), [twelve controls and 234 finite targets](../../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/mathematical-abstraction/acquisition/README.md), [review](../../../research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/ABSTRACTION-ACQUISITION-REVIEW.md) and conventional learning antecedents preserve funded failures, false bounds, bad reset and miscalibrated semantics. The old predictor supplies ancestry/resources, not the new model's content. This completes a conditional acquisition interface; architecture origin, warranted experimental access, bound/meaning revision and empirical identification remain open. [Ren approved this direction](../../../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-06-abstraction-acquisition-direction.md); unique mathematical ontology remains a separate unproved hypothesis.

**Experimental access from an acquired model, 6 October 2026:** [the completed conditional interface](../../../../theories/shengsheng-buxi-generative-existence/unified-2026-10-05/EXPERIMENTAL-ACCESS.md) carries the actual retained three-state model and remaining resources into resource-dependent tests of sensor, readiness and reset assumptions. Exact sensor symmetry preserves two explanations; a calibrated consequence receipt separates them only under declared transfer access. [Hand proofs](../../../research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/EXPERIMENTAL-ACCESS-MATH.md), [independent review](../../../research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/EXPERIMENTAL-ACCESS-REVIEW.md) and [144 family runs plus 18 fixtures](../../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/mathematical-abstraction/acquisition/access/README.md) retain false-premise diagnoses, capacity failures and consumed material. An exact factored representation admits blocked transfer without discarding alternatives; 36/48 probe cases retain results and 12 fail final retention. The fault family, diagnosis recipe and consequence calibration remain supplied. Next is retention of acquired evidence using available resources or reacquisition for model-family revision; the external audit cannot become free learner memory. This advances the accepted derivation without establishing unique mathematical ontology or completing the unified foundation.

**Model-family revision through fresh evidence, 6 October 2026:** [the completed conditional continuation](../../../../theories/shengsheng-buxi-generative-existence/unified-2026-10-05/MODEL-FAMILY-REVISION.md) constructs an enlarged candidate from the actual retained model, reacquires current behavior and pays to retain and use the selected representation. [Proofs](../../../research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/MODEL-FAMILY-REVISION-MATH.md) characterize when memory permits retrospective reinterpretation; the executable deliberately takes a conservative fresh audit instead. [Independent checks](../../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/mathematical-abstraction/acquisition/access/revision/README.md) cover 14 inherited configurations, 14 ample-resource configurations, 14 named fixtures and six renamings. Eight inherited configurations complete; six fail later use or retention. The actual earlier mistaken branch can afford a refuting observation but cannot install revision. Candidate grammar, audit policy, current-family laws, external changes and tariffs remain supplied. This supporting mathematics bridge closes current-family reacquisition, with raw-evidence retention unbuilt. Next on this branch is consequence-sensitive formation and selection of candidate generators and retention policies; the central concern/wisdom derivation and five completion criteria remain unchanged.

**Revision-policy feasibility boundary, 6 October 2026:** [the completed boundary audit](../../../../theories/shengsheng-buxi-generative-existence/unified-2026-10-05/META-REVISION.md) follows actual model-family revision endpoints without new support or a hidden reward. Both named primary branches can confirm another prediction but cannot fund its final retention or the previous installation recipe. Two other endpoints have more resources, so the limit is not universal. [Independent checks](../../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/mathematical-abstraction/acquisition/access/revision/metarevision/README.md) cover all eight retained endpoints, a wrong accepted model, twelve derived payment boundaries and 256 logical memory/decoder designs. [Proofs](../../../research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/META-REVISION-MATH.md) establish exact future-question memory requirements and finite-coverage limits without excluding useful constrained retention policies. No metaselector is built. The next positive supporting bridge is payable operational demand/feedback → changed retained selector → enacted generator or retention procedure, with renewed support for sustained operation. Existing inference, reward and scheduling criteria remain supplied; the central concern/wisdom connection and five completion criteria are unchanged.

**Mathematical truth: remaining-argument resolution, 6 October 2026:** [the completed continuation](../../../../theories/shengsheng-buxi-generative-existence/unified-2026-10-05/MATHEMATICAL-TRUTH-AND-THE-ROOT.md#resolution-of-the-remaining-bridges) establishes canonical closure and generated identity under explicit rules, and exact semantic determination under a fixed interpretation. The supporting [model-admissibility study](../../../research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/MATHEMATICS-MODEL-ADMISSIBILITY.md) gives the precise classical consistency/model, truth-profile and noncategoricity distinctions. Strong countermodels show that leastness does not select its rules, compositional closure does not select Boolean logic, finite iteration does not supply full infinite closure, and necessary truth's extensional dependence cannot identify its unique ground. A proposed generative structural identity interpretation supplies a scoped philosophical bridge; its identification with the uniquely ultimate causal root is not derived. [Review](../../../research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/MATHEMATICS-REVIEW.md#remaining-bridges-resolution) closes this argument assessment while preserving the unrestricted metaphysical hypothesis and the unified foundation as unachieved. The further substantive target is a specific independently justified closure or identity law, not another inference from generic composition or truth agreement. Earlier arithmetic and universal set-sized first-order presentation results remain in force; no new empirical or machine-checked mathematics is claimed.

**Mathematics and the strongest generative claims, 6 October 2026:** the [deeper investigation](../../../../theories/shengsheng-buxi-generative-existence/unified-2026-10-05/MATHEMATICS-AND-GENERATIVITY.md#how-far-the-claims-can-extend) establishes a canonical coarsest exact abstraction for specified deterministic observations and a finite certificate for all finite continuations under a known state bound and exact reset/query interface. [Nine hand propositions](../../../research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/MATHEMATICS-STRUCTURAL-RESULTS.md) and [exhaustive checks of 9,994 constructed machines](../../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/mathematical-abstraction/README.md) accompany generative-presentation arguments, a foundation audit and five primary empirical comparisons. The [integration/review](../../../research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/MATHEMATICS-REVIEW.md#deeper-claim-audit-and-structural-development) retains standard antecedents, the failure of finite tests without a state bound, and interpretation/infinity/self-justification limits. The next bridge is an actually formed learner acquiring diagnostic evidence, refining an abstraction and supplying an interpreted, affordable certificate. Logic, calibration, question relevance, state bound and learning machinery remain supplied. The claim audit is complete at this scope; no new Lean or empirical validation, unique ontology or completion of the foundation is claimed.

**The actual predictor now satisfies a paid protocol contract, 6 October 2026:** [the concrete bridge](../../../../theories/shengsheng-buxi-generative-existence/unified-2026-10-05/PREDICTION-PROTOCOL.md) connects its rent, work, write and finish events to independently stated command, update and resource equations. **Thirty-eight new Lean audits** establish exact completed finite histories, actual consumed-receipt reports, one source packet per trial and intermediate accounts. The macro description keeps full physical boundary states; recovering a receipt from old/new memory does not construct a runtime evidence channel. Paid entry/write failures, cleared receipts, expired updaters and a changed next command remain explicit. The [separate review](../../../research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/PREDICTION-PROTOCOL-REVIEW.md) accepts the deterministic payable-write trial scope. Reversal/reformation histories, full stochastic execution, component realization of the retrofit and the origin/revision of action routing remain further connections.

**Paid implementation across event scales, 6 October 2026:** [protocol composition](../../../../theories/shengsheng-buxi-generative-existence/unified-2026-10-05/PROTOCOL-COMPOSITION.md) extends the controller interface from single matching events to completed sequences of physical events. Thirty-nine new Lean audits establish flattening through actual boundary states, physically decoded memory under a local implementation contract, prefix resource accounts and internal-rank bounds. Paid examples preserve finite source depletion, unaffordable entry and an actual paid deadlock. Two implementations have exactly the same completed finite macro histories while one admits infinite internal stalling under the same committed command. The [review](../../../research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/PROTOCOL-COMPOSITION-REVIEW.md) accepts the finite implementation interface; the subsequent concrete predictor bridge above establishes its trial contract, while completion, probability and timing require further premises.

**Issued-operation completion, 6 October 2026:** [the completion contract](../../../../theories/shengsheng-buxi-generative-existence/unified-2026-10-05/PROTOCOL-COMPLETION.md) now adds actual next-event availability, preservation and strict internal rank to the completed-history interface. Thirty-four new Lean audits establish bounded completion of maximal admitted operations, continuation through actual returned states and separate prefix accounts. The unchanged predictor trial completes exactly four events under its explicit guards; refusal, paid deadlock, stalling and depleted readmission remain visible. [Separate review](../../../research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/PROTOCOL-COMPLETION-REVIEW.md) accepts this conditional contribution to compatible composition. It supplies no elapsed-time, unrestricted-scheduler or perpetual-renewal guarantee. The central successor, warranted revision and empirical identification work remains ongoing.

**Constructed evidence retention, 6 October 2026:** the [recording integration](../../../research/2026-10-05-concern-theory/RECORDING-FIDELITY-INTEGRATION.md) joins actual variant formation to paid bit slots, preallocated writing and faithful finite evidence. Thirty-three new Lean audits establish the event/trace contract; independent closed-form checks reproduce all 298 finite endpoint rows. Selective omission followed by resumed sampling raises a false-certificate probability from 1/65536 to about 0.567529, despite paying every trial bill; permanent stopping preserves a genuine prefix. The supplied sampling law, recipe, finite no-erasure interval and completion schedule remain explicit. This supplies a finite record premise, not a confidence computer or renewable memory. The next connection carries identified evidence through revision of the prediction-to-action coupling.

**Noisy learning and the concern decision, 6 October 2026:** the [new reliability integration](../../../research/2026-10-05-concern-theory/TRANSFER-EVIDENCE-INTEGRATION.md) replaces repeated exact numerical teaching with explicit stationary-evidence conditions. Constant-gain noisy learning cannot generally maintain an eventual all-date positive choice margin; retained counts can support eventual choice or an anytime signed-score certificate under separate assumptions. [Five exact comparisons](../../../../experiments/shared-renewal-capability/regulation/retention/formation/learning-interface/transfer-evidence/README.md) reuse the actual paid teacher and charge a later recipient action, with independent enumeration of 209,495 paid words and 680 coverage checks. They retain stopping bias, an unaffordable certified action, stale evidence after change and the cost of conservative withholding. Calibration, shared routing, correspondence meaning, growing memory and composition with formed updater machinery remain supplied or unresolved. These are mathematical and executable results, not new Lean declarations or empirical validation.

**Preserving the learner across descriptions, 6 October 2026:** [controlled refinement](../../../../theories/shengsheng-buxi-generative-existence/unified-2026-10-05/CONTROLLED-REFINEMENT.md) connects exact world-history representation to the existing observation-driven controller. A world mapping that preserves complete observations, with the same controller and inherited memory, preserves and lifts finite controlled histories; compatible interfaces can be chained. Fifteen new Lean audits include a counterexample where all uncontrolled action possibilities and fuel costs match but erasing a sensed bit changes the controller's next command. This supplies a composition criterion for the parallel formation and predictive-learning branches, not a proof that their different material laws already coincide. Shared action granularity, channel/memory compatibility and separately justified budgets remain required; probability transport and architecture origin remain open. The [review](../../../research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/CONTROLLED-REFINEMENT-REVIEW.md) records the scoped result and a separate proposed concern-identification diagnostic.

**A shared material history for selectors and updaters, 6 October 2026:** [material updater composition](../../../../theories/shengsheng-buxi-generative-existence/unified-2026-10-05/MATERIAL-UPDATER-COMPOSITION.md) connects the declared updater law to actual typed constituents with **39 new Lean theorem audits**. A coarse updater history lifts exactly when it is feasible and its combined updater/selector demands fit both inherited stocks. One actual preparation funds the full changed-updater history; subsequent reconstruction can remain affordable while exhausted components block it. Finite stocks bound new updater formations even with abundant fuel. The [independent model and review](../../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/learning/construction/updater/README.md) retain sequential competition, the stronger free-part adapter and the failed two-material entrance. This supplies a common consumable implementation under declared recipes, not equivalence to recyclable slots or transfer of earlier probabilities. **Next:** connect formed machinery to acquired, corrected prediction and paid use under this same material law; provide actual production or reclamation if unbounded reconstruction is claimed.

**Material construction of a changing selector, 6 October 2026:** [the joined material interface](../../../../theories/shengsheng-buxi-generative-existence/unified-2026-10-05/MATERIAL-SELECTOR-COMPOSITION.md) adds [40 Lean theorem audits](../../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/learning/construction/README.md). An existing selector history lifts to its material realization exactly when it is feasible and its cumulative demands for each part type fit inherited stocks. An actual prior formation/repair/capture/copy history supplies those stocks and the fuel for the earlier reversal witness. A checked wrong-first path reaches the correct mapping with source remaining but insufficient prepaid fuel to use it. The [independent review](../../../research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/CONSTRUCTED-LEARNING-REVIEW.md) accepts this finite staged construction and its accounting, with supplied recipes, packet access and maintenance coupling kept explicit. This closes a conditional part-to-selector interface; simultaneous chemistry and origins of the wiring/update laws remain further obligations. The subsequent material-updater result supplies the finite shared-component interface.

**Paid teaching and the concern interface, 6 October 2026:** the [integration](../../../research/2026-10-05-support-and-shared-generativity/completion/CONSEQUENCE-TO-LEARNING-INTEGRATION.md) works backward from the concern model's supplied teaching target. A [measured dependency and finite prediction construction](../../../../experiments/shared-renewal-capability/regulation/retention/formation/learning-interface/README.md) checks when a target is accessible, can be written before action, and can support the stated learning recurrence. Its independent audit covers twelve gate preparations, four primary stochastic cases, 390 parameter cases and 45 decay cases. Insufficient reserve changes the acquired histories; even a fully funded mean above an action threshold leaves half the histories below or at it. The result supplies an operational prediction interface, not felt valence, shared evaluation or a joined derivation of concern. Sensor/updater formation, sustained calibration and pathwise downstream reliability remain explicit obligations.

**Shared accounting extension:** [FormationAccounts.lean](../../../../experiments/shared-renewal-capability/regulation/retention/formation/formal/FormationAccounts.lean) now reuses the parallel `FormationSupport` bridge, adding fifteen checked interface declarations for common-fuel isolation, reaction/leakage separation, exact boundary cancellation and same-state composition. An enabled zero-fuel-cost damage event prevents misapplication of the general positive-unit-demand theorem. These deductions share the existing law and proof dependencies; they are not independent empirical evidence or fifteen separately established mechanisms.

**Accessible revision and actual support, 6 October 2026:** the [learning interface](../../../../theories/shengsheng-buxi-generative-existence/unified-2026-10-05/LEARNING-INTERFACE.md) adds [48 Lean theorem audits](../../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/learning/README.md). For any supplied deterministic controller, equal inherited memory and complete feedback histories give equal commands and final memory; incompatible future-continuation requirements therefore cannot both be met by an indistinguishable next choice. A separate formal bridge instantiates the universal support laws on the actual formed-variant process, including local capture necessity and the spent-resource handoff after repair. These close information and resource interfaces toward selector revision. The fixed-regime witness does not establish controller/channel formation, recovery after reversal, predictive learning or concern. The parallel costed-selector result addresses a conditional reversal mechanism; composition with architecture formation remains open. The [internal review](../../../research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/LEARNING-INTERFACE-REVIEW.md) retains these boundaries and overlap with concurrent same-law accounting work.

**Conditional universality, 6 October 2026:** following [Ren's law-class refinement](../../../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-06-conditional-universality-request.md), the [new general account](../../../../theories/shengsheng-buxi-generative-existence/unified-2026-10-05/CONDITIONAL-UNIVERSALITY.md) derives continuation constraints for every process satisfying explicit local laws. Its [60-theorem Lean package](../../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/conditional/README.md) proves cumulative demand/provision bounds, their transport through step-preserving maps, executable accounted-cycle repetition, and finite deadlines for finite-lived supports with bounded strictly increasing dependency ranks. Checked witnesses separate possible renewal from depletion and unused cycles. Of the new audits, 34 have empty dependencies; the others use only standard `propext`/`Quot.sound`, with no proof holes or custom axioms. The [separate internal review](../../../research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/CONDITIONAL-UNIVERSALITY-REVIEW.md) accepts the scoped results. Independently identified accounts, lifetimes, parent relations and boundary conditions remain inputs. Unique metaphysical necessity is no longer a prerequisite for the universal target. The next mechanism bridge remains formation and revision of selectors; these bounds constrain its supports without supplying learning or a reward.

**Deeper substrate-to-dynamics layer, 6 October 2026:** [the deeper generative root](../../../../theories/shengsheng-buxi-generative-existence/unified-2026-10-05/DEEPER-GENERATIVE-ROOT.md) integrates Ren's common-root/plurality proposal with a [32-declaration Lean extension](../../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/deeper/README.md). Product iteration preserves seed emptiness and absence of distinctions; identical carriers admit incompatible dynamics; supplied steps generate a unique least endpoint reachability relation; every supplied prefix-closed finite event-word language containing the empty word has an exact history-state realization. All new reported axiom dependencies are empty. This closes a conditional substrate/continuation analysis, not a premise-free ontology or universal physical derivation. The [genealogy](../../../research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/DEEPER-ROOT-GENEALOGY.md) and [internal review](../../../research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/DEEPER-ROOT-REVIEW.md) preserve the stronger failed inferences and their repairs. The next shared mechanism task remains formed alternatives → differential retention → selector revision; the deeper result clarifies which laws that work legitimately supplies.

**Lean core, 6 October 2026:** the [formal realization and scope](../../../../theories/shengsheng-buxi-generative-existence/unified-2026-10-05/GENERATIVE-CORE-LEAN.md) now make finite-continuation completeness a necessary-and-sufficient local criterion. The [checked implementation](../../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/README.md) includes actual-successor composition, a natural-reserve obstruction, reachable revision/renewal/loss witnesses, and complete versus incomplete state summaries. This closes a precise mathematical representation question and demonstrates a coherent realization of the candidate's causal commitments. Its transition mechanisms remain supplied; the origin of regulation and revisable learning remains the next explanatory bridge. The [internal review](../../../research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/LEAN-CORE-REVIEW.md) separates these statuses.

### Physical formation, renewal and directional comparisons

**Three-session integration, 6 October 2026:** the [reconciliation and interface map](../../../research/2026-10-05-repository-reconciliation/three-session-integration/README.md) accounts for all 856 external substantive files from the three requested chats and accepts the pending physical-renewal package. The concern settlement and joint-formation results were already preserved and accepted; the unified reading path now makes their completed scope clearer. Source copies and previous proofs retain their evidence status.

**Latest accepted physical contribution:** [repeated-renewal composition](../../../research/2026-10-05-support-and-shared-generativity/completion/REPEATED-RENEWAL-COMPOSITION.md) retains actual successors, time and support expenditure. The [rate-derived critique](../../../research/2026-10-05-support-and-shared-generativity/completion/REPEATED-RENEWAL-CRITIQUE.md) bounds n complete turnovers in the current four-slot unit-rate architecture by (3/4)^n even with stronger fuel support. An [observer-level Lean proof](../../../research/2026-10-05-support-and-shared-generativity/completion/CARRIER-TURNOVER-FORMALIZATION.md) strengthens carrier-loss accounting. A conditional mixing witness shows how identical marginal populations can conceal different joint consequences. The [RNA evidence audit](../../../research/2026-10-05-support-and-shared-generativity/completion/RNA-REPEATED-RENEWAL-EVIDENCE.md) clarifies the transfer measurement needed; its linked three-cycle assay remains unrun. The [review](../../../research/2026-10-05-repository-reconciliation/three-session-integration/PHYSICAL-BRANCH-REVIEW.md) accepts these supporting results without claiming that they derive significance, learning or wider concern.

The following work develops one essential part of the foundation: how actual processes form and renew capabilities, and when their dynamics favor shared continuation. Its [specification](../../../../theories/shengsheng-buxi-generative-existence/unified-2026-10-05/FORMAL-SPECIFICATION.md) and [completion plan](../../../../theories/shengsheng-buxi-generative-existence/unified-2026-10-05/COMPLETION-PLAN.md) retain that branch's directional target. Its stronger positive hypothesis remains under investigation within the wider explanatory program, alongside the origin of significance and learning.

**Previous first-replacement advance:** [complete first replacement](../../../research/2026-10-05-support-and-shared-generativity/completion/FULL-SUCCESSOR-RESULT.md) now follows every formation history into tagged carrier turnover, with directed probability bounds. The comparison changes sign when the first successor must restore inherited reserve. Both events remain extremely rare. The [RNA partial-identification result](../../../research/2026-10-05-support-and-shared-generativity/completion/RNA-PARTIAL-IDENTIFICATION.md) certifies a conditional conventional joint prediction and distinguishes irrelevant microscopic ambiguity from missing joint observations. Specification v0.9 and the [review](../../../research/2026-10-05-support-and-shared-generativity/completion/FULL-SUCCESSOR-REVIEW.md) retain physical calibration, reliable repeated renewal, broad capabilities and universal direction as open obligations.

**Previous entrance and discovery advance:** [complete formation entrances and successor composition](../../../research/2026-10-05-support-and-shared-generativity/completion/FULL-ENTRY-SUCCESSOR.md) retain all formation histories, with certified first-entry probabilities and explicit inherited reserves. The [Lean extensions](../../../../experiments/shared-renewal-capability/formal/README.md) now check all 42 typed reactions and reserve-sensitive turnover. [Consequential discovery](../../../research/2026-10-05-support-and-shared-generativity/completion/CONSEQUENTIAL-DISCOVERY.md) gives a conditional paid-search guarantee for omissions that produce no old-data anomaly. The [RNA identification contract](../../../research/2026-10-05-support-and-shared-generativity/completion/RNA-IDENTIFICATION-CONTRACT.md) reconstructs published effective coefficients and identifies missing measurements for a joint replacement task. Specification v0.8 and its [review](../../../research/2026-10-05-support-and-shared-generativity/completion/ENTRANCE-DISCOVERY-REVIEW.md) retain the open full-successor, physical, capability and universal-direction obligations.

**Previous paid-access milestone:** [complete succession](../../../research/2026-10-05-support-and-shared-generativity/completion/PAID-ACCESS-SUCCESSION.md) has a certified unit-coefficient comparison whose sign reverses with the deadline. Its [rate-class and finite-lineage limits](../../../research/2026-10-05-support-and-shared-generativity/completion/SUCCESSION-DIRECTION-CRITIQUE.md), [physical constraints](../../../research/2026-10-05-support-and-shared-generativity/completion/PAID-ACCESS-PHYSICAL-CONSTRAINTS.md) and [separate reversible certificate](../../../research/2026-10-05-support-and-shared-generativity/completion/PAID-ACCESS-THERMODYNAMIC-CERTIFICATE.md) remain preserved. Registered other-chat contributions have been reviewed; the [application contribution](../../../research/2026-10-05-support-and-shared-generativity/completion/APPLICATION-INTEGRATION.md) supplies implementation evidence only.

**Prior formation/turnover components:** [physical turnover](../../../research/2026-10-05-support-and-shared-generativity/completion/RENEWAL-RETURN.md), [material fabrication](../../../research/2026-10-05-support-and-shared-generativity/completion/ARCHITECTURE-FORMATION-CRITIQUE.md), [first-capture reversibility](../../../research/2026-10-05-support-and-shared-generativity/completion/THERMODYNAMIC-EMBEDDING.md), and [direction limits](../../../research/2026-10-05-support-and-shared-generativity/completion/DIRECTION-AND-TURNOVER.md) remain preserved with their original scopes.

**Preceding advance:** [paid allocation control](../../../research/2026-10-05-support-and-shared-generativity/completion/ALLOCATION-CONTROL.md) proves a finite-rate class in which construction from the same supported zero-gate state improves joint access to fresh support, after an early construction disadvantage. The [continuation assay](../../../../experiments/shared-renewal-capability/continuation/README.md) retains the old regulator's repeated-delivery failures. The [cellular baseline](../../../research/2026-10-05-support-and-shared-generativity/completion/RESOURCE-ALLOCATION-BASELINE.md) supplies an established resource-allocation comparison.

**Formalization-led completion of this branch:** the [common specification](../../../../theories/shengsheng-buxi-generative-existence/unified-2026-10-05/FORMAL-SPECIFICATION.md) and [practical completion plan](../../../../theories/shengsheng-buxi-generative-existence/unified-2026-10-05/COMPLETION-PLAN.md) connect physical renewal, joint capability, derived stochastic transitions and actual formation. The [integration record](../../../research/2026-10-05-support-and-shared-generativity/completion/INTEGRATION.md) tracks completed contributions and active turns awaiting review. This is ongoing research; its stronger positive directional hypothesis remains open.

The new [mechanism-to-capability bridge](../../../research/2026-10-05-support-and-shared-generativity/completion/JOINT-CAPABILITY-BRIDGE.md) derives joint probabilities directly from the shared-renewal event law. It proves positive finite-time construction effects and computes incomparable installed capability regions at a common reliability. The [completed joint-formation review](../../../research/2026-10-05-support-and-shared-generativity/completion/JOINT-FORMATION-INTEGRATION.md) accepts the strategic formation theorem with its physical and equilibrium limits.

The [physical regulator extension](../../../../experiments/shared-renewal-capability/regulation/README.md) now pays for formation, binding, wear and replacement within that process. Its first prespecified comparison is negative: construction costs overwhelm the proposed regulator's benefits in every tested comparison with the absent-regulator arm. A correct sensing response and a stronger energy source do not by themselves establish joint generative advantage. The [concern-settlement source review](../../../research/2026-10-05-support-and-shared-generativity/completion/CONCERN-SETTLEMENT-INTEGRATION.md) separately accepts the completed strategic contribution and preserves the unrun empirical-study status.

The [opportunity audit](../../../research/2026-10-05-support-and-shared-generativity/completion/REGULATION-OPPORTUNITY-AUDIT.md) and [independently checked response diagnosis](../../../../experiments/shared-renewal-capability/regulation/response/README.md) explain why useful regulation needs a specified productive conflict. Recycling stores usable material; less free fuel alone is not less later production. The [full biological feedback audit](../../../research/2026-10-05-support-and-shared-generativity/completion/PHYSICAL-EVIDENCE-CERONI.md) supplies an external burden-and-relief mechanism, including costly failure designs and source-data limitations. Next are delivered-continuation assays, a physically derived gain/failure boundary and an identified comparison with conventional resource-allocation models; universality remains open.

### Significance, learning and concern

The candidate foundation describes activity changing the conditions of further activity. Its ethical development asks how another being's condition becomes a reason within a process, how that reason can learn and widen, and how effective concern survives costs, obstruction, and succession. Supporting conditions, recognition, valuation, justified standing, operational influence, and actual protection have distinct roles. Their origins and connections are central derivation tasks under the governing position above.

The completed studies supply conditional mechanisms and counterexamples. They do not establish a universal tendency toward wiser or more inclusive regeneration. The current [theory overview and genealogy](../../../../theories/shengsheng-buxi-generative-existence/README.md) keeps that distinction explicit.

## Preservation and future work

The [reconciliation record](../../../research/2026-10-05-repository-reconciliation/README.md) explains what was found and repaired. Its [source manifest](../../../../sources/22-research-reconciliation-2026-10-05/MANIFEST.json) maps inspected external files to exact repository bytes, including duplicates, preserved originals and explicit exclusions. Earlier snapshots remain intact. A [location map](../../../../sources/22-research-reconciliation-2026-10-05/LOCATIONS.md) helps locate material by its former session folder.

Continue substantive work in the existing repository study, following [AGENTS.md](../../../../AGENTS.md). Update this index as a branch advances; link to repository files in deliveries. Keep exported bundles as snapshots and retain original provenance paths in historical records. Preservation, link repair and organization do not add evidence for a scientific or ethical claim.
